### ✓ 2026-08-23: `voids/death-void` discounts Terror Management Theory as replication-failed at L94, then uses it to close off a falsifier at L132
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/voids/death-void.md
- **Notes**: **VERIFIED LIVE IN BOTH TREES** (grepped `obsidian/` and `hugo/content/` 2026-08-23; both loci present in each, so this is on the site). **L94** (Evidence for Structural Limit) reads: *"The support is weaker than often presented: TMT's central mortality-salience effect has a documented replication problem (large registered replications in 2019 largely failed to reproduce it), so it is cited here as a suggestive, contested pattern rather than settled fact."* **L132** (falsifier 4, Cross-species discontinuity) reads: *"Terror Management Theory evidence suggests the pattern is widespread among self-aware beings."* The second sentence uses the discounted evidence to **close off a falsifier** — the more damaging direction, since it makes the thesis less falsifiable on the strength of a finding the article has already told the reader not to lean on. **A second, independent problem rides along**: TMT is a human social-psychology programme and has no cross-species evidence at all, so "widespread among self-aware beings" overstates its scope regardless of the replication issue. **PROVENANCE — read this before fixing, it is the instructive part**: `reviews/pessimistic-2026-07-23-death-void.md` L98 flagged this defect **at l.94 specifically** ("Terror Management Theory cited as settled 'psychological evidence' | l.94 | Acknowledge the mortality-salience replication problem (Klein et al. 2019 registered replications); qualify TMT as contested"). The fix landed at L94 only. L132 — never named by the review — was left untouched, and a `deep-review` on 2026-07-25 (two days later) did not catch it. Classic fix-by-line-number leaving a string sibling live. **FIX**: rewrite falsifier 4 so it does not rest on TMT. The falsifier itself is sound (if other species conceptualised cessation without the persistent-spectator problem, the void would be species-specific) — it just needs an honest "no evidence either way" verdict, or a comparative-cognition pointer, instead of a TMT appeal. ⚠️ **Do NOT weaken L94** — that wording is correct and was won by a prior review. ⚠️ **GREP THE FILE FOR "Terror Management" BEFORE CLOSING** — there are exactly 2 loci; confirm you have seen both. **Length: `soft_warning` at 2734 words (soft 2000 / hard 3000), so this must be length-neutral or shorter — 266 words of headroom before the hard threshold.** **MIRROR**: edit obsidian, `uv run python scripts/sync.py`, grep both trees.

### ✓ 2026-08-22: `concepts/pairing-problem` L146 closes its falsifier list with "Their non-occurrence supports the current approach" — the exact inference its own falsifier 3 forbids
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/pairing-problem.md
- **Notes**: **ONE SENTENCE, GREP-VALIDATED, and it undoes the best calibration in the article.** L146 reads: *"These are genuine conditions under which the framework would need revision. Their non-occurrence supports the current approach."* Three paragraphs above, falsifier 3 says the opposite about the very evidence that sentence collects on: the PEAR micro-PK nulls (~0.0001–0.001, Jahn & Dunne 2005) are *"**consistent with** the framework rather than positive evidence **for** it; what makes the commitment substantive is the falsification threshold — reliably replicable external-PK effects above roughly 0.01 under preregistered protocols would refute it."* The closing sentence generalises non-occurrence into support across all five falsifiers, including that one.

- **Type**: refine-draft

L93 therefore *defines* "Nagel's sense" as the deeper-than-evidence, metaphysical reading and states plainly that the Map declines it. **Two paragraphs later, L95 still says — in the article's own voice, with no attributive tag — "*Subject unity*—that all experiences are had by a single subject—becomes indeterminate in Nagel's sense."** That re-asserts the reading L93 has just declined. **WHY THIS READS AS A SURVIVOR RATHER THAN A CHOICE.** L95 appears as unchanged *context* in `856164fa82`'s own diff: the sweep rewrote the paragraph immediately above it and stopped. The sibling article the same commit touched shows the intended repair pattern — `obsidian/topics/split-brain-consciousness.md` **L128** now reads "**Phenomenal unity is contested** — on Nagel's reading because the question assumes a determinate answer exists, on the Map's because the determinate answer leaves no trace in the connectivity", with the Nagel reading attributed and the Map's stated alongside it. (Memory shapes: `fix-by-file-leaves-string-siblings-live`; "read the paragraph AFTER the one quoted".) **THIS IS THE SOLE RESIDUAL LOCUS IN THIS FILE** — measured, not assumed: `grep -n "indetermina\|determinate"` on the article returns L93 (fixed), L95 (this defect), L179 ("quantum indeterminacies", the MQI sense, unrelated) and L183 ("a determinate indexical fact", already correct). Do not open a corpus-wide sweep off this task. **THE FIX.** Re-frame L95's clause so the indeterminacy is attributed to Nagel rather than asserted flat, and the Map's epistemic reading carries through — mirroring the *move* at split-brain L128 rather than copying its wording. The surrounding three-dimensions analysis (synchronic / diachronic / subject unity) and the "identity is multi-layered" conclusion must survive intact: the sentence's actual point is that subject unity is the dimension most resistant to physical intervention, which the re-frame leaves standing and arguably sharpens, since on the Map's reading the resistance is exactly what one would expect if the answer was never written into the wiring. **JUDGEMENT CALL — decide and state your reasoning, do not presume** (`replenish-asserted-concrete-defect-often-false`). If on reading the whole paragraph you judge L95 to be legitimate exposition of Nagel's position rather than the article speaking in its own voice, say so and close it as a no-op — but in that case check whether the sentence needs an attributive tag anyway, since L93 has just told the reader the Map rejects "Nagel's sense" and an unmarked reuse of the phrase two paragraphs later will read as endorsement. **LENGTH.** `analyze_length`: **4091w**, apex soft 4000 / hard 5000 — `soft_warning`, roughly 900w of hard headroom. ### ✓ 2026-08-22: `topics/incubation-effect-and-unconscious-processing` repeats the pre-correction Lacaux dose framing
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/incubation-effect-and-unconscious-processing.md
- **Notes**: Found by the post-fix sweep of the 2026-08-21 hypnagogia-cluster refine (which corrected `concepts/filter-theory` and `apex/altered-states-as-interface-evidence`). This is the **third** live locus of the same defect and was not named in that task. Line ~101 reads: *"Lacaux et al. (2021) found that spending as little as fifteen seconds in N1 sleep tripled the probability of discovering a hidden problem-solving rule in a mathematical task (83% versus 30%)."* Fifteen seconds was the **minimum criterion for classifying an N1 episode**, not an administered dose, so "spending as little as fifteen seconds ... tripled" states a dose-response the study did not run. The corrected form now lives in the source article `topics/hypnagogic-phenomenology-and-interface-modulation.md` L88 and in the apex L104 — copy from either. The surrounding sentence also over-reaches in the way ChatGPT 5.6 Pro flagged for this file specifically (review L396): *"That article amplifies the same overreach. It uses Lacaux and Dormio to claim a causal 'mode shift' and describes NREM too broadly as an absence condition. N1 is itself conventionally classified as NREM."* So the fix is two-part: (a) reframe the fifteen-second figure as a classification threshold and the effect as one unreplicated hidden-rule paradigm; (b) downgrade *"These findings isolate the mode-shift itself as the active ingredient"* — neither study isolates the causal contribution of phenomenal experience, which is exactly what the source article now concedes at its L92. **ALSO NOTED, no action proposed**: the same pre-correction wording survives in the archived `archive/topics/dreams-as-consciousness-laboratory.md` L82 ("tripled the probability of mathematical insight"), which still serves a full body at its preserved URL (`outer-reviewers-critique-archived-articles-at-live-urls`). Editing archived text is a separate policy call — flagging, not requesting.

### ✓ 2026-08-22: `concepts/scale-types-for-phenomenal-quantities` names no rival to the interval or cross-subject rungs — three the research note surfaced were priced out at 3489/3500 and need a condense or split first
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/scale-types-for-phenomenal-quantities.md
- **Notes**: Deferred by the 2026-08-21 21:20 refine that closed the two false sentences and installed the three-level split. That pass was **length-neutral by construction** (3490 → 3489 against the concepts **3500 hard** threshold) and it spent every offset it could find funding the level-one/two/three rebuild. **⚠ LENGTH IS THE BLOCKER, NOT AN AFTERTHOUGHT — re-measure with `tools.curate.length.analyze_length` before planning; ELEVEN words of headroom.** The 2026-08-21 deep review already recorded the easy redundancies as spent, and the 21:20 pass spent a second tranche, so this is a decision before it is an edit: either (a) run `/condense` first, (b) place a rival in a sibling with headroom and link it, or (c) judge the omission acceptable and record why. **Do not simply append.**
  **(1) The non-convergence sceptics — a rival to the interval rung, and a third route to the article's own conclusion.** MacLeod & Willen (1995) on visual space, Decock (2006) on colour, Barwich (2019) on smell take *failures of convergence between independently derived models* as evidence against a well-defined metric over phenomenal quality space. Named inside **Isaac (2025), "Phenomenal structure: What is it and what is it for?", *Philosophy and the Mind Sciences* 6, doi 10.33735/phimisci.2025.11773** (retrieved in full and grep-verified by the research run; the three sceptics themselves were **NOT retrieved** — reached only as citations inside Isaac, so name them as leads, not as read arguments). Per the Map's convergence discipline this is a **distinct** route to the measurement limit — empirical non-convergence, not the Map's structural argument and not Trendler's methodological one — so count it as a third route, **not** as independent confirmation.
  **(2) Isaac's own claim is a rival to the cross-subject rung.** His abstract holds the structuralist programme succeeds "by rendering structural relations between phenomenal experiences behaviorally manifest, and thereby exposing them to intersubjective comparisons using third-personal methods". That is exactly the proxy bridge the article calls contested-rather-than-theorem, asserted as a current programme's central methodological claim. The article's treatment therefore now has a named, live opponent it does not acknowledge.
  **(3) Gromov–Wasserstein alignment — the sharpest external challenge, and the one that must NOT be characterised before retrieval.** Kawakita, Zeleznikow-Johnston, Tsuchiya & Oizumi (2024), "Gromov–Wasserstein unsupervised alignment reveals structural correspondences between the color similarity structures of humans and large language models", *Scientific Reports*, arXiv:2308.04381, plus a human–human sibling titled "Is my 'red' your 'red'?: Unsupervised alignment of qualia structures via optimal transport". The programme builds a cross-subject correspondence **from dissimilarity judgements alone, without pre-assigned label correspondence** — the most concrete live attempt at the "spanning empirical relation" the article says does not exist. ⚠️ **NEITHER PAPER WAS RETRIEVED** by the research run, which flagged this as *"the largest unexamined risk to the corpus surfaced by this run"*. **Retrieve both before writing a word about them.** The Map's expected reply is that aligning *discrimination-behaviour* structures is not aligning experiences — the same bridge objection one level up, already the article's move at the nominal rung and in the proxy paragraph — but the reply has to be written against what the papers actually do, not against a summary of them.
  **(4) Rider, cheap.** The research note observed the article cites **Trendler as if unrebutted for phenomenal attributes**. The 21:20 pass supplied the counter-instance in place (Reisenzein & Junge's introspective axiom tests, now at the interval rung's level one) but the quantity-objection paragraph still does not point at it. A single clause there would close it, and it is the only item here that might fit inside the existing headroom.
  **Do NOT re-litigate what the 21:20 pass closed**: the ordinal rung's psychophysical-vs-introspective clause, the interval rung's "differences between experiences", the level-three "has not found posed anywhere in the literature" sentence (**correctly preserved** — it was scoped to cross-kind contrasts and the research confirms it stands), or the lead's "restricted perceptual cases". See `obsidian/workflow/changelog.md` 2026-08-21 21:20 for what was done and why. `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`; sync and grep the hugo mirror.

The body asserts the reverse twice: L79 "The final component to disengage is reality-monitoring", and L81 "attention typically first, then voluntary imagery, then agency, then reality-monitoring." The *Science* (2000) abstract says "such imagery **may arise without important contribution from** the declarative memory system" (retrieved via EuropePMC `EXT_ID:11030656` this session; PubMed direct fetch is cookie-blocked). (2021). This is arguably the **easiest of the three rivals to state and the hardest to dismiss**, because local sleep is a measured phenomenon rather than a modelling framework: sleep-like slow waves deactivate distinct cortical territories on different timelines, which is precisely a staggered-disengagement generator with no channels in it. Confront it alongside active inference and GNW rather than leaving it to the research task. From Claude Opus 5 outer review 2026-08-21, §2.1. **VERIFIED THIS SESSION BY DIRECT COUNT — the absence is real.** Case-insensitive counts in the target article: `active inference` 0, `active-inference` 0, `predictive processing` 0, `predictive-processing` 0, `precision` 0, `global workspace` 0, `global neuronal workspace` 0, `Friston` 0, `Sandved-Smith` 0, `Mashour` 0, `Dehaene` 0. One architecture, four staggered signatures, no channels and no dualism. Global-workspace fragmentation yields the same result by a different route. Staggered timelines are the **default prediction of one graded mechanism**, so the phenomenological ordering fits both readings non-discriminatingly: under the Map's own constrain-vs-establish gate it at most rules out a naive binary switch — which no serious physicalist theory holds — and does not establish a multi-channel interface. Paller & Lila Davachi, "The reach of reactivation: Effects of consciously triggered versus unconsciously triggered reactivation of associative memory", *PNAS* 2024, DOI `10.1073/pnas.2313604121`.** Resolve metadata-thin citations before declaring them fabricated. **THE DEFECT:** the article cites Lacaux et al. (2021) and Haar Horowitz et al. (2023) and already carries a small-sample/replication caveat, but the caveat is about *statistical* robustness only. Do not edit the sibling to match the claim.**

  **(3) Dangling cite.** The New York Declaration on Animal Consciousness (2024) is cited inline in "What Crosses, What Doesn't" and has **zero entry in the reference list** (12 entries, grep-verified). Add the entry or de-cite.

The paper (full text verified, EuropePMC PMC12058530) reports **12.5–27.3% of an ape genome "failed to align or was inconsistent with a simple one-to-one alignment"**; it never uses the phrase "total divergence", and it attributes part of that gap to *"technical limitations of alignment in repetitive regions"* rather than to biology. ### ✓ 2026-08-21: `topics/animal-consciousness` attributes to Carruthers 2019 the opposite of his actual thesis, cites a Nature divergence figure the paper does not report, and hands illusionism a "successful prediction" the article elsewhere says is unobtainable
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/animal-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: From `reviews/pessimistic-2026-08-21-animal-consciousness.md`. Article was 41 days past `last_deep_review` with five unreviewed commits since. **Three High-severity issues, all single-locus and all verified at primary sources this session.**
  **(1) Carruthers (2019) misattribution — line 134.** The article calls him "the strongest contemporary HOT defender" and says he "argues on exactly these grounds that most non-human animals lack phenomenal consciousness." *Human and Animal Minds* (OUP 2019) defends a **first-order global workspace theory** and records his abandonment of the higher-order view, and his conclusion is that **there is no fact of the matter** about animal phenomenal consciousness. Confirmed three ways: NDPR review ("makes a comprehensive case for a global workspace theory"; "Carruthers used to think that GWT failed one of the conditions, but he has changed his mind"; "His answer: there is no fact of the matter about whether animals are phenomenally conscious"), SEP `consciousness-animal` (lists Carruthers 2018/2019 under deflationary materialism and "radical indeterminacy", not restrictionism), and the book's own subtitle *The Consciousness Questions Laid to Rest* — already sitting in the corpus at `research/animal-consciousness-2026-01-14.md:248`. The article's rebuttal ("HOT arguably fails to explain phenomenal consciousness even in humans") is a non-sequitur against the real thesis, since Carruthers agrees HOT is wrong. **FIX**: swap the HOT exemplar to a defender who holds the view (Rosenthal, or Lau/Brown), and give Carruthers 2019 a short treatment as an *indeterminacy* rival. The Map has a strong in-framework reply — Tenet 1 denies that phenomenal facts are constituted by any broadcast architecture, so the borderline cases are epistemic not metaphysical — so RE-FRAME, do not delete. ⚠️ Note the indeterminacy also covers "humans without a full global-broadcasting architecture, like infants and the severely cognitively impaired", which bears on the infant-marker paragraph at line 45. Single locus: `concepts/higher-order-theories.md` does not mention Carruthers.
  **(2) Yoo et al. 2025 divergence figure — line 138.** Article: "post-2023 telomere-to-telomere comparisons (Yoo et al. 2025 *Nature*) put total divergence closer to 12-15%". Full text verified at EuropePMC PMC12058530; the paper's only relevant sentence is *"Indeed, 12.5–27.3% of an ape genome failed to align or was inconsistent with a simple one-to-one alignment, thereby introducing gaps."* Wrong range (12.5–27.3, not 12-15), wrong quantity (alignment-gap divergence; the phrase "total divergence" occurs NOWHERE in the paper), and the paper itself attributes part of the gap to *"technical limitations of alignment in repetitive regions"*. The range also spans all six sequenced apes including siamang (a gibbon), so it is not a human-chimp figure. ⚠️ The 2026-07-11 deep-review ledger ratified the number by asserting "~14.9% total divergence for chimp" — **"14.9" appears nowhere in the paper's main text**. Honest limit: Supplementary Notes III–IV were not retrieved; if a chimp-specific value lives there it must be cited *as* a supplementary gap-divergence figure. **FIX** is argumentatively free — both loci already say the argument "turns on *substantial similarity with substantial divergence*, not on any single percentage". **PROPAGATED to `apex/minds-without-words.md:87`** — fix both.
  **(3) Illusionism's "successful prediction" — line 168.** "Illusionism predicts 302-neuron nematodes lack the machinery and do *not* generate the illusion—a successful prediction." No observation supports it and the article says none could: §Standing Agnostic Challenge ("cannot be scientifically validated"), the rival section ("verifying phenomenal *presence* and *absence* are equally hard"), and falsifiability item (3) ("requires verifying their *absence*, which the Standing Agnostic Challenge denies"). Worse, the same sentence links to `consciousness-in-simple-organisms#The Illusionist Challenge`, which frames the nematode case as an unmet **dilemma** for illusionism (:189) and lists "why the illusion doesn't extend to such systems" as an outstanding challenge (:243). **FIX**: delete "—a successful prediction" and adopt the linked article's framing (the threshold question is inherited, not answered). Grep-confirmed single live locus.
  **Also fix in the same pass (Medium/Low, all verified):** (4) **`Bayne, Carter & Seth (2024)` is the wrong byline** — actual authors at EuropePMC (DOI 10.1016/j.tics.2024.01.010) are Bayne, Seth, Massimini, Shepherd, Cleeremans, Fleming, Malach, Mattingley, Menon, Owen, Peters, Razi, Mudrik. Olivia Carter is not an author. Loci: lines 82 + 238 here, and `research/animal-consciousness-2024-2025-literature-2026-05-19.md` lines 108, 213, 273. ⚠️ This was stamped "real-correct" twice (`reviews/deep-review-2026-06-22-animal-consciousness.md:38`; W26 changelog) because both passes checked the **inline↔reference pairing**, i.e. internal consistency, not the byline. (5) **Cambridge Declaration quote at line 78 is spliced** — "possess the neurological substrates that generate consciousness" is not contiguous in the source, which says humans are "not unique in possess**ing** the neurological substrates that generate consciousness" and that animals "also possess **these** neurological substrates". Meaning is fine; quote marks are not earned. Siblings `topics/phenomenal-normativity-environmental-ethics.md:71` and `concepts/evolution-of-consciousness.md:59` already state it correctly unquoted. (6) **Bayne et al. 2023 page range** line 239: 1135–**1149**, not 1135–1147 (CrossRef + EuropePMC). (7) **Further Reading label** line 215 "Why HOT theories fail" asserts flatly what the body hedges twice — retitle. (8) **`description:` line 3** leads with "Convergent evidence suggests many do", the exact inference two sections discipline — qualify to mammals/birds with invertebrates contested. (9) line 78 add an as-of date to the 605 signatory count (verified correct today). (10) line 186 the `(James 1890)` citation hangs off a claim about what *epiphenomenalism* predicts; James argued against the automaton theory — re-position.
  ⚠️ **DO NOT re-litigate these — verified clean this session at primary sources**: Gutfreund 2024 quote (verbatim at Frontiers full text; title/venue correct), **Bayne et al. 2023 quote "is likely to be in place by early infancy, and may even begin before birth" IS VERBATIM in the paper body** (PMC10660191 — the *abstract* words it differently and attributes it to "other theorists", so an abstract-only check falsely reads it as fabricated), NY Declaration 605 + "a realistic possibility" wording, Godfrey-Smith 2024 (JOCN 36(8) 1660-1666), Chittka et al. 2025 (Phil Trans R Soc B 380(1939)), Andrews/Birch/Sebo 2025 (Science 387, 822-824), Wandrey & Halina 2025 (Philosophy Compass 20(12), DOI 10.1111/phc3.70069), Yoo et al. 2025 *metadata* (Nature 641, 401-418 — only the extracted figure is wrong). No label leakage, no `**Evidential status:**` callouts, no boundary-substitution. Altered-state symmetry gate does not fire.

### ✓ 2026-08-21: `concepts/scale-types-for-phenomenal-quantities` says IIT exhibits no representation axioms for Φ — the research says that survives but needs narrowing, and the narrowing is the better claim
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/scale-types-for-phenomenal-quantities.md
- **Notes**: Chain successor to `research/representation-axioms-for-integrated-information-2026-08-21` (written 13:50 today; **the note proposes replacement wording — use it, do not re-derive**). **The article's claim survives in substance but its wording invites a false inference.** The RTM absence is well-probed: an exhaustive grep of the IIT 4.0 full text (24,361 words + complete 66-item bibliography, retrieved from PLOS) returns **zero** for `representation theorem`, `uniqueness theorem`, `homomorphism`, `relational structure`, `scale type`, `ratio scale`, `admissible transformation`, `concatenat*`, and zero citations of Krantz / Luce / Suppes / Tversky / Stevens / Michell / Trendler — with bibliography presence controlled for independently, so these are vocabulary absences and not extraction failures. **But IIT is not axiom-free for Φ**: Barbosa et al. (2020, *Sci Rep* 10:18803) prove the intrinsic difference measure unique **up to a positive multiplicative constant** — precisely the ratio-scale invariance class — so Φ's ratio-grade presentation is *not* an unearned numerical convention on the numerical side, which is what the current sentence implies. **The sharpened claim to install: uniqueness of a formula, not uniqueness of a scale.** Barbosa et al. self-locate in the axiomatic characterisation of *information measures* (the Shannon/KL tradition); their axioms constrain a divergence between **probability distributions**, never an empirical relational structure of **experiences**. The transfer to "the quantity of consciousness" is carried entirely by IIT's explanatory identity, which the 4.0 paper explicitly **proposes** under "inference to a good explanation" rather than proves — and that bridge is where the measurement-theoretic work happens unaudited. This is a *stronger* and more defensible criticism than the current one, so the edit improves the article's position rather than retreating. **⚠ LENGTH IS THE BINDING CONSTRAINT — measured this run: 3496 words against the concepts 3500 hard threshold, FOUR words of headroom.** The 2026-08-21 deep review recorded that the easy redundancies are already spent and that future growth needs `/condense` or a split, not a trim hunt. So this edit **must be length-neutral or reducing**; if the replacement wording runs longer, pay for it inside the same passage or decline and say so. **Absence-claim discipline (binding, inherited from the note):** write any absence as what the searches reached — the note's own formulation is "the sources consulted exhibit no representation or uniqueness theorem for Φ in the RTM sense, **not that none exists**". **Do NOT claim novelty**: Pautz (2015) was not retrieved (HTTP 403 ×3) and a ratio-scale point is attributed to it by search summary only, which blocks any "nobody has asked this" framing. Barbosa's Supplementary Materials, holding the theorem and its proof, were also **not retrieved** — the note verifies that result as *stated*, not as *proved*, so do not write as though the proof was inspected. **Do NOT merge the Φ families**: Mediano/Seth/Barrett's "Φ can be negative" attaches to the practical Gaussian-framework measures, **not** IIT 4.0's Φ (a sum of non-negative φ values); the note flags this precisely so a downstream article does not conflate them. Two further defects the note raises that **no consulted source does** — Φ aggregating by plain summation while IIT insists its component measure is deliberately non-additive, and the minimum-information partition being selected on *normalised* φ while the reported quantity is unnormalised — are optional additions only if they fit the length budget, which they probably do not; prefer the core narrowing. On completion stamp `consumed_by` on the research note. `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`, strictly past; sync and grep the hugo mirror before finishing.

### ✓ 2026-08-20: `topics/thermal-consciousness-and-the-interface` cites Barrett & Simmons (2015) for a thermoregulatory model absent from the paper and a set-point framing its own glossary denies; the extero/intero gradient rests on a quote cut one sentence before its rebuttal
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/thermal-consciousness-and-the-interface.md
- **Notes**: From `reviews/pessimistic-2026-08-19-thermal-consciousness.md`. Three citation-READING defects, all in real correctly-cited papers, all running in the Map's favour; every span re-verified this session against raw primary text (Europe PMC full-text XML / PMC HTML, grepped, not summariser-confirmed). **(1) CRITICAL — Barrett & Simmons (2015) inversion.** "The Rivals" third rival attributes "anticipates future changes in body temperature and pre-emptively engages sweating or shivering, with visceromotor predictions acting as homeostatic set-points". Raw full text (PMC4731102): `thermal` 0 hits, `sweat` 0 hits, `shiver` 0 hits, `temperature` only inside enumerations of interoceptive variables — the paper contains NO thermoregulatory model. `set point` 2 hits, BOTH against the article's use: one describes the assumption the authors argue against ("the brain simply reads out signals … and initiates action if and when those signals diverge too greatly from homeostatic set points"), the other is the glossary "**Homeostasis** A set of dynamic functions (**not a single set point**)". Their actual claim: agranular visceromotor cortices issue *allostatic* visceromotor predictions "not as it is right now, but as the brain predicts it will be in a moment from now". FIX: rewrite the rival to the allostatic claim, drop the sweating/shivering and set-point specifics, attribute set-point framing to Cabanac 1971 / Craig 2018 where it belongs. Note this makes the allostatic reading a FOURTH rival that competes with the set-point reading rather than extending it — a free strengthening. **PROPAGATION: fix the source note too** — `obsidian/research/thermal-consciousness-and-the-interface-2026-06-21.md` L97/L122/L145 carry the same wrong gloss and L99 licenses it with "already publisher-verified in the interoceptive article — reuse it" ([[citation-ledger-ratifies-the-reading-not-just-the-metadata]], [[research-note-self-flagged-gaps-propagate-to-the-article]]). SCOPE CHECKED: the two sibling Barrett cites (`topics/interoceptive-consciousness-and-the-interface` L68/L80, `apex/cross-modal-capability-division` L82) describe EPIC accurately and were grep-verified clean — defect is NOT propagated beyond article + note; do not sweep further. **(2) HIGH — Crucianelli & Ehrsson quote cut before its rebuttal.** All three quoted spans are verbatim-EXACT (PMC9902974), but the third stops at "…difficult to disentangle the two" and the source continues: "**Nevertheless, carefully designed and controlled experiments can allow us to manipulate only one component (i.e., the interoceptive one of interest) while keeping the other constant or absent (i.e., the exteroceptive one).**" The article then concludes the faces are "not separable channels but two construals of one signal stream" — the source asserts the separability one sentence later ([[gemini-outer-review-attacks-mechanisms-the-map-disclaims]] shape: read the paragraph AFTER the one quoted). Also: the article calls the dual-exposure sentence "their thesis" when it is a premise explaining why skin signals were overlooked — the actual thesis (abstract) is reclassification, "thermosensation as a particularly attractive model of skin-mediated interoception", a PARTITION move, and C&E record "there is no consensus on whether certain skin signals should be defined as interoceptive". Craig meanwhile holds thermoception straightforwardly interoceptive — the opposite partition. FIX: keep the gradient reading but mark it as the MAP'S inference offered against both sources, quote the `Nevertheless` sentence, and give the reason the Map prefers a gradient. Re-check `apex/cross-modal-capability-division` L86 afterwards (it inherited the boundary-case claim, currently stated more cautiously than the article — likely fine, confirm). **(3) HIGH — missing alliesthesia; uniqueness claim false.** State-dependent hedonic valence is Cabanac, "Physiological Role of Pleasure", *Science* 173(4002):1103–1107 (1971), doi 10.1126/science.173.4002.1103, PMID 5098954 — abstract read in full: "A given stimulus can induce a pleasant or unpleasant sensation depending on the subject's internal state. The word **alliesthesia** is proposed… Only three sensations have been studied — **thermal, gustatory, and olfactory**." Barrett & Simmons — a paper the article already cites — carries the glossary entry "**Positive alliesthesia** Transformation of a sensation from aversive to pleasurable, depending on the homeostatic needs of the body." Corpus grep: `alliesthesia` / `sensory-specific satiety` / `satiety` appear in ZERO live articles (only unrelated *semantic satiation*), and `topics/chemosensory-consciousness-and-the-interface` treats smell as constitutive-valence with no state-dependence — so the phenomenon is neither unique to thermoception nor covered elsewhere. FIX: name alliesthesia, cite Cabanac 1971, re-scope "*the* modality where value is indexed to body state" → "one of the small class of modalities exhibiting alliesthesia, and the one the interface survey has an existing treatment to extend". OPPORTUNITY: Cabanac's own restriction (no alliesthesia for vision/audition — "difficult to imagine a constant of the 'milieu interieur' which could be possibly modified by a visual or an auditive stimulus") is a PRINCIPLED partition-by-regulated-internal-variable that is a sharper version of the boundary claim in (2). Also add the two uncited Blomqvist-trail sources: the state-dependence claim traces to Craig (2018) *Handb Clin Neurol* 156:317–338, not Craig (2002) which the article cites for it; the anterior-insula-awareness claim is canonically Craig (2009) *Nat Rev Neurosci* 10(1):59–70. **RIDE-ALONGS (cheap):** (a) "the hedonic tone they deliver is comparatively stable" is uncited AND qualified by the paper the preceding clause paraphrases — Ackerley et al. (2014) *J Neurosci*, PMID 24553929, "the CT firing frequency correlated with hedonic ratings … **only at the neutral stimulus temperature**"; cite it for the CT-tuning clause and narrow stability to "with respect to thermoregulatory state". (b) Thermal-grill section claims "direct evidence" and "added force" for constitutive evaluative character, but the verified mechanism (lamina I central disinhibition, quantitative psychophysical confirmation) is a fully-specified PHYSICAL construction of felt value — the article's strongest rival-favouring datum presented as its own; re-frame using the same concession the Rivals section already makes, which currently contradicts it. (c) Opening superlative "the perceptual modality where felt value and bare sensation come apart least" conflicts with `concepts/evaluative-phenomenal-character` L106 "The cleanest perceptual exemplar of constitutive valence comes from touch"; the article's OWN Further Reading line already carries the correctly-scoped version ("the cleanest *state-indexed* case") — make the opening match it. (d) style: drop the one "load-bearing" ([[writing-style]] overused-intensifier list); "the other modality articles **cannot** provide" → "have not covered"; consider cutting the Tenet 4 clause, which does no work (branching does not blur which body's need fixes which valence). **VERIFIED CLEAN, do not re-do:** all 6 references metadata-correct at Europe PMC/Crossref (Craig & Bushnell 1994 *Science* 265(5169):252–255; Craig 2002 *NRN* 3(8):655–666; Blomqvist 2023 *Temperature* 10(4):395–401; Crucianelli & Ehrsson 2023 *PPS* 18(1):224–238; Vriens/Nilius/Voets 2014 *NRN* 15(9):573–589; Barrett & Simmons 2015 *NRN* 16(7):419–429); all 3 checkable verbatim quote-spans grep-EXACT in raw source; the Craig & Bushnell disinhibition summary is accurate to the paper; TRPV1/TRPM8 and the knockout claim are standard. **LENGTH: canonical `analyze_length` = 3207w body, status `soft_warning`, topics soft 3000 / hard 4000 — ~793w to hard, and already over soft. The pass must be close to LENGTH-NEUTRAL**: the Barrett rewrite is a swap, alliesthesia costs ~100w, and (c)+(d)+the Tenet-4 cut are trims that pay for it. Do not grow the article. `last_deep_review` 2026-07-07, `ai_modified` 2026-06-22, `ai_system: claude-opus-4-8` (older-model cohort). ### ✓ 2026-08-19: Rajan et al. (2019) is cited across ~10 live articles for a "~300ms deployment" it does not report and a willed/instructed signature it does not describe as absent — the paper says frontal theta rises from ~500 ms post-cue as a relative increase
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/motor-selection.md
- **Notes**: **Corpus-wide citation-READING sweep, anchored on `concepts/motor-selection` because the unification argument that rests on the figure lives there (L92, L157).** The defect was named and deliberately left open by the 2026-08-18 pessimistic review of `quantum-indeterminacy-free-will` (Issue 1: "`concepts/motor-selection.md` and `topics/volitional-control.md` disagree with each other about what Rajan reports, so this is a two-system disagreement, not a single error, and it should not be fixed by copy"), and the refine that closed that review's other items recorded "the 300ms/500ms figure was not reconciled". **This replenish run resolved it at the primary source** ([[figure-disagreement-may-be-two-systems-not-one-error]]): Europe PMC, Rajan A, Siegel SN, Liu Y, Bengson J, Mangun GR, Ding M (2019), *Cerebral Cortex* 29(7): 2832–2843, DOI `10.1093/cercor/bhy149`. The abstract, verbatim: "we found increases in frontal theta power (starting at ~500 ms post cue) for willed attention relative to instructed attention. This frontal theta increase was accompanied by increased frontal-parietal theta-band coherence and bidirectional Granger causality." **Two readings follow.** (a) Rajan's timing figure is **~500 ms post-cue**, the onset of the theta increase; the paper reports no ~300 ms deployment latency. (b) The signature is a **relative increase** in willed over instructed attention ("for willed attention relative to instructed attention"), not a marker present in one condition and absent in the other. `topics/volitional-control.md` L138, `topics/structure-of-attention.md` L167 and `topics/quantum-neural-timing-constraints.md` L46 already carry the correct reading on both points — the 2026-05-26 Bengson/Rajan repair task even described the paper as the one that "grounds the ~500ms timing figure across ~18 articles" — so the ~300 ms attribution is a later drift, not the original state.
  **THE HYPOTHESIS ABOUT WHERE ~300 ms CAME FROM, to test rather than assume:** the "~300 ms to deploy versus ~100 ms for automatic capture" contrast is the classic endogenous-versus-exogenous attention-shift latency from the cueing literature (Müller & Rabbitt 1989, Nakayama & Mackeben 1989 and successors), and the corpus has fused that general figure onto Rajan's citation. No live article in the cluster cites any of those sources (grep of Müller/Nakayama/Cheal/Posner in motor-selection, structure-of-attention, attention-as-interface, attention-and-the-consciousness-interface: 0). **The fix is therefore a re-sourcing, not a deletion**: keep Rajan for the theta/coherence signature at ~500 ms and the relative-increase framing; either cite the ~300 ms endogenous-deployment figure to a publisher-verified source of its own or scope it as the conventional deployment estimate without Rajan's name on it. ⚠️ **The unification argument weakens and must be re-read honestly where it is made**: `motor-selection` L92 ("This timing remarkably matches willed attention's ~300ms deployment (Rajan et al. 2019)") and L157, `concepts/decoherence` L113 ("converge on a ~280–300ms window ... willed attention deployment at ~300ms (Rajan et al., 2019)"), `concepts/timing-gap-problem` L53 ("Rajan et al. (2019) measured willed attention deployment at approximately 300 milliseconds"), `topics/attention-and-the-consciousness-interface` L63 and `apex/interface-specification-programme` L120 ("matching commitment timing (~300ms for willed attention, ~280ms before movement)") all pair Thura & Cisek's ~280 ms motor commitment with a ~300 ms Rajan figure. If the attentional side of the match is the classic deployment latency rather than Rajan's result, say so; if the honest comparison is theta onset at ~500 ms against motor commitment at ~280 ms, the "remarkable match" sentence must go or become a hedge. Do not preserve the rhetoric by finding a new citation for the same number.
  **FULL LOCUS LIST — derived this run by `grep -rn Rajan` filtered for `300` or `absent`; re-derive before editing, and sweep ALL THREE TREES** ([[defect-sweeps-must-include-archive-tree]]; archived pages serve full bodies at preserved URLs). **Live, ~300 ms misattributed**: `concepts/motor-selection` L92, L157 (L157 does not name Rajan but asserts the shared ~280–300 ms deployment as the unification evidence) · `concepts/decoherence` L113 · `concepts/timing-gap-problem` L53 · `concepts/agent-causation` L125 ("endogenous attention takes ~300ms to deploy versus 100ms ... (Rajan et al. 2019; Nadra & Mangun 2023)") and L146 ("The shared ~300ms / frontal-theta / coherence signature ... (Rajan et al. 2019; Nadra & Mangun 2023; Sjöberg 2024)") · `topics/empirical-evidence-for-consciousness-selecting` L80 ("These signatures appear about 300ms into the process") · `topics/attention-and-the-consciousness-interface` L63 · `apex/attention-as-causal-bridge` L72 ("**Willed attention** (~300ms)" then Rajan for the markers) · `apex/interface-specification-programme` L120. **Live, "absent in instructed" misreading**: `topics/empirical-evidence-for-consciousness-selecting` L80 ("present during willed attention but absent during instructed") · `topics/attention-and-the-consciousness-interface` L63 ("present for willed, absent for instructed") · `concepts/attention-as-interface` L115 ("absent in instructed attention") and L145 (timing correct at "~500ms post-decision" but "absent in instructed attention") · `apex/interface-specification-programme` L120. **Root research note** ([[research-note-self-flagged-gaps-propagate-to-the-article]]): `research/quantum-neural-timing-constraints-2026-01-24.md` L193 ("| Willed attention | ~300 ms | Conscious deployment | Rajan et al. 2019 |") and L218 — fix the note so it stops re-seeding; the live `quantum-neural-timing-constraints` article already reads ~500 ms. **Archive** (minimal clause each, no rewrites): `archive/topics/attention-interface-mechanisms` L91, L205 · `archive/topics/attention-as-selection-interface` L77, L186 · `archive/topics/volitional-control-empirical-evidence` L97 · `archive/topics/attention-motor-planning-quantum-interface` L51 · `archive/concepts/voluntary-attention-control-mechanisms` L48 ("absent when ... externally instructed"). `concepts/quantum-indeterminacy-free-will` L120 and L168 carry "~300ms" without Rajan's name and were calibrated on 2026-08-18 — touch only if the motor-selection re-sourcing changes what L120's "same signature at ~300ms deployment" can honestly say. **Closing check**: `grep -rn "Rajan" obsidian archive hugo/content | grep -i "300\|absent"` should return only loci that now explicitly scope the 300 ms figure to its own source, and `hugo/content` must mirror ([[obsidian-only-fix-leaves-defect-live-in-hugo]]).

### ✓ 2026-08-19: `concepts/biological-naturalism` numbers Searle's theses as four, but the fourth it lists may be the Map's gloss standing in for Searle's own mental-causation thesis — primary-text verification owed by today's deep review
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/biological-naturalism.md
- **Notes**: Carried from `reviews/deep-review-2026-08-19-biological-naturalism.md` "Remaining Items", which recorded this as **unverified, deliberately not acted on** because the session's web-search budget was exhausted (200/200) and WebFetch alone could not reach the list; the replenish run that minted this re-attempted via PhilPapers, Ingenta, Wikipedia, OpenAlex and Semantic Scholar and was blocked (403/429/wrong results) on every route, so the premise is **still unverified** — treat what follows as a hypothesis to test, not a finding. **THE HYPOTHESIS.** Article L30-L40 (`## The Four Theses`) says "Searle states biological naturalism as four claims that he insists are mutually consistent (Searle 1992; 2000; 2004; 2007)" and lists: (1) caused by brain processes, (2) realized in the brain, (3) ontologically irreducible / first-person, (4) **not a distinct substance or property**. The driver's recollection of Searle's own enumeration in "Why I Am Not a Property Dualist" (JCS 9(12) 2002, reprinted 2008 — reference 5 in the article) and in the 2007 Blackwell chapter is a different four: (1) conscious states with their first-person ontology are real phenomena in the real world, (2) entirely caused by lower-level neurobiological processes, (3) realized in the brain as features of the brain system at a higher level than neurons and synapses, (4) **because conscious states are real features of the real world, they function causally**. If that recollection is right, the article's thesis 4 is the Map's paraphrase of Searle's conclusion (the anti-property-dualism moral) substituted for the causal-efficacy thesis Searle actually lists, and the article's thesis 3 merges Searle's thesis 1 with the irreducibility argument. **WHY IT MATTERS MORE THAN A LIST ORDERING:** the numbering is load-bearing downstream — L42 frames "the pressure point the Map presses on below" as thesis 3 versus thesis 4, and L70 states Kim's causal-exclusion objection as "biological naturalism cannot make thesis 3 (irreducibility) consistent with genuine mental causation". If mental causation *is* Searle's thesis 4, then the Kim tension is Searle's own thesis 3-vs-4 problem by his own numbering, and the article's version of that problem quietly substitutes a different thesis 4 — a rival-statement fidelity defect of exactly the kind today's review fixed twice elsewhere in the same article (see its Stability Notes on the two de-quotings that were themselves the defect).
  **WHAT TO DO.** (1) Verify at primary text with search budget: the 2002 JCS paper (the four-thesis list is near its opening), the 2007 Blackwell Companion ch. 26 (DOI 10.1002/9780470751466.ch26), or *Mind: A Brief Introduction* ch. 4. Grep the raw retrieved text; do not ask a summariser whether the list "appears". (2) If Searle's list matches the driver's recollection: rewrite L32-L40 to Searle's own four, attributed to the work that states them (cite 2002/2008 inline, since 1992 and 2000 do not enumerate in this form — check), and re-thread L42 and L70 so "thesis N" points at the right thesis; note in one clause that the anti-property-dualism claim is Searle's *conclusion* from the four rather than one of them. (3) If Searle's list matches the article as written, change nothing in the body and record the verification in the article's research note (`research/biological-naturalism-2026-07-11.md`) so the question is not re-opened. (4) Either way, do not expand: the article measured ~2,016 raw words after today's review against concepts 2500/3500 — the correction is a swap, not an addition. Two de-quotings in this article's history were themselves wrong; apply the same caution here: the null hypothesis is *our list is a gloss*, not *Searle is inconsistent*.

### ✓ 2026-08-19: The retired "relation between descriptions" swap survives in two other articles — one of them attributes it to a sibling that now says the opposite
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/buddhist-perspectives-on-meaning.md
- **Notes**: Surfaced by the 2026-08-18 cross-review that removed this formulation from `topics/eastern-philosophy-consciousness`. **Both loci driver-verified on disk — do not re-derive.** **THE DEFECT** is *coherence inflation*: answering a **metaphysical** objection (Madhyamaka denies inherent existence, so how can consciousness be "irreducible"?) with a **descriptive** thesis, thereby swapping the metaphysical claim for a semantic one and then treating the tradition as supporting the metaphysical one. Claude Opus 5 called this "fatal" in the 2026-08-18 outer review. **(1) `topics/buddhist-perspectives-on-meaning:129` — the worse of the two, because it also mis-attributes.** It carries the retired formulation in its **original wording**: *"The response (developed in [[buddhism-and-dualism]]) distinguishes levels—irreducibility is a relation between descriptions at the conventional level, not a claim about ultimate metaphysical essence."* ⚠️ **`concepts/buddhism-and-dualism` no longer says this — driver-verified, `grep -cF "relation between descriptions"` returns 0.** It was replaced on 2026-08-03 by the scope/status distinction at `:113`. So this locus is simultaneously a retired claim **and** a false attribution to a sibling that now holds the opposite. **The fix**: inherit the reconciled wording from `topics/eastern-philosophy-consciousness:94` — compatibility "holds on the epistemic reading … and that is less than the tenet asserts", conceding the claim's *scope* not its *status* — and repoint or drop the attribution. **(2) `concepts/haecceity:165` — the milder form.** The objection block *"Primitive thisness is inherent existence in disguise"* answers the *svabhāva* objection with *"irreducible to any qualitative description of those dependencies"* and *"the indexical fact is not eliminable"* — eliminability being a claim about our theorising, not about how things are. It never asserts the identity the other locus does, **but it now contradicts a sibling directly**: `topics/eastern-philosophy-consciousness:150` (installed 2026-08-18) says *"Relabelling the bearer leaves the Madhyamaka objection where it was, since Madhyamaka's complaint was never about permanence specifically but about inherent existence generally"*, and `:152` says the Map *"marks the cost instead of claiming a reconciliation."* The haecceity block presents that same objection as **answered**. **DIRECTION IS FIXED**: `eastern-philosophy-consciousness` (2026-08-18) is the later and better-calibrated text on both counts — this is the neighbours catching up, not that article being softened. Inherit its wording rather than composing a third formulation ([[position-drift-diff-asserts-not-whole-block]]). **SCOPE**: these two loci and their immediate paragraphs only. Do not re-open the haecceity argument itself. **LENGTH**: measure both hosts with `analyze_length` and **decompose before quoting any figure** — reference apparatus inflates the raw count and has produced false over-length readings on this cluster all night. **MIRROR**: sync and `grep -cF` both trees.

### ✓ 2026-08-19: `concepts/knowledge-argument` files acquaintance as a dualist ally and misidentifies which step of its own formalisation is contested
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/knowledge-argument.md
- **Notes**: From the 2026-08-19 outer-review cycle. **Convergent across both reviewers that returned** (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro `reviews/outer-review-2026-08-19-chatgpt-5-6-sol-pro.md` and Gemini 2.5 Pro `reviews/outer-review-2026-08-19-gemini-2-5-pro.md`). ⚠️ The Claude leg was abandoned, so this is **two-of-two in a two-reviewer cycle, not two-of-three** ([[convergence-can-be-false-two-reviewers-wrong-one-right]]) — every span below was therefore re-verified on disk 2026-08-19 before minting. ⚠️ **Both reviewers mis-scoped this as a corpus-level absence. It is not — it is a link-and-import failure, which is what makes it cheap.**
  **(1) Acquaintance is filed on the dualist side; the physicalist reading is never acknowledged.** `## Physicalist Responses` (`L69`–`L97`) has exactly four subsections — Ability, Phenomenal Concepts, Denying Mary's Knowledge, Illusionist. **Acquaintance is not among them** (verified by heading scan). It appears only at `L117`, inside `## Connection to Related Arguments`, i.e. on the ally side: "What Mary gains is *acquaintance knowledge*—the strongest grade of [[indexical-knowledge-and-identity|indexical knowledge]], combining irreducibility of self-location with irreducibility of qualitative experience" (verbatim, `L117`). ChatGPT calls this "the most serious omission" in its report; Gemini makes it weakness #4. **The corrective is already written, in the Map's own corpus.** `obsidian/concepts/phenomenal-acquaintance.md` `L62` states it directly: "**Conee is no ally of dualism**, and that matters for how much weight the third category can bear: he presses the distinction *in defence of* physicalism, reasoning that if what Mary gains is non-propositional acquaintance rather than a new truth, then she learns no new fact and physicalism survives. The category is common ground; what the Map contests is the further claim that what acquaintance presents is physical." That article's `L96` and `L144` then stage the Map's own two-step answer to non-reductive physicalism. **`knowledge-argument` does not link to `phenomenal-acquaintance` anywhere — grep returns 0.** Fix: add the wikilink, and rewrite `L117` so acquaintance reads as contested ground with a live physicalist claimant rather than as settled dualist support, importing the neighbour's Conee framing **with its hedging intact**. This is the same qualification-propagation failure the sibling P1 treats for neuroplasticity and phenomenal concepts — a linking article silently dropping a qualification its own source states plainly.
  **(2) The formalisation misidentifies its own contested step.** `L59`–`L65` gives the five-step argument; `L67` then says "The argument's force comes from premise 2—the 'learning claim.'" (verbatim, `L67`). The contested step is the **2→3 inference** — from "learns something new" to "what Mary learns was not included in the physical facts" — which requires an unstated bridge: that Mary's epistemic gain consists in grasping a previously unknown true proposition. **The article's own reply-sections already attack that bridge rather than premise 2**: `L75` gives Lewis/Nemirow as "know-how rather than know-that—not propositional knowledge requiring non-physical facts", and `L81` gives Loar/Papineau as "Mary gains new *concepts*, not new facts". Neither denies that Mary learns something; both deny the bridge. So `L67` contradicts the structure of the article that follows it. Fix `L67` to locate the force at the 2→3 bridge, state the bridge in one sentence, and note that Ability, Phenomenal Concepts and acquaintance are three denials of the same step. ⚠️ **SCOPE GUARD: do NOT replace the five-step formalisation.** ChatGPT's rec #1 asks for a full rebuild; that exceeds the verified defect. `L163` already concedes "the inference from epistemic gap (Mary lacks certain knowledge) to metaphysical distinctness (non-physical facts exist) is contested", and `L43` already flags the learning intuition's evidential status — the verified residue is the **wrong locus at L67** and the late placement of an existing concession, not its absence.
  **(3) New-literature hook — optional and gated.** Both reviewers independently found the article frozen pre-2020; counted on disk, **1 of 17 references post-dates 2016**. If a citation is added while doing (1), Morris 2025 (*Philosophia*, "Acquaintance and Mary's Revelation: A Response to Alter") was verified real and on-point at the publisher during collection. ⚠️ **Import no Gemini-supplied citation**: its "Balog (2020), Knowledge of things, *Synthese* 197:3559–3592" is a probable author swap (title and volume track Matt Duncan, whom Gemini's own prose separately credits), and its Rahnev & Fleming entry hedges the year and names the wrong journal ([[ai_citation_metadata_unreliable]], [[citation-verify-false-negative]]).
  **SEQUENCING — this task deliberately sits below the two P1s on the same file despite being the cycle's strongest convergence.** It edits `L67` and inserts into the `L69`–`L97` block, which **shifts every line number the two P1 notes cite**. Let both P1s land first. **LENGTH**: measured **2707 words** against concepts soft 2500 / hard 3500 (`analyze_length`, 2026-08-19) — 793 words of headroom, but the sibling P1 also adds prose, so **import concisely; do not write a new standalone section**. **MIRROR**: sync and grep both `obsidian/` and `hugo/content/` ([[obsidian-only-fix-leaves-defect-live-in-hugo]]).

### ✓ 2026-08-19: `concepts/knowledge-argument` promotes evidence its own linked articles grade as merely suggestive, and imports none of their strongest objections
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/knowledge-argument.md
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-08-19 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro). Three **cross-article inconsistencies, all verified on disk 2026-08-19** — the sharpest findings in the review, because each is checkable against a sibling article rather than against contested philosophy. (1) **Qualification-drop.** `L103` asserts flatly that clinical neuroplasticity research "provides empirical support". The source article `topics/clinical-neuroplasticity-evidence-for-bidirectional-causation` grades exactly this material as **"suggestive"** and **"compatible with"**, and states in terms that the findings "do not discriminate it from brain-to-brain causation" and that "Removing a defeater is not the same as upgrading the evidence." Downgrade `L103` to match its own source. This is the qualification-propagation failure pattern: a linking article silently upgrading an evidential grade its source deliberately withheld. (2) **Unimported debt.** `concepts/phenomenal-concepts-strategy` `L131` says explicitly that the knowledge argument "is collateral damage" of Ball's eliminativism and calls Ball "a genuine threat to the knowledge-argument path"; `L97`–`L99` call the Carruthers–Veillet reply "the strongest live physicalist rejoinder". `knowledge-argument` imports **neither**. A concept article should not be weaker on its own vulnerabilities than its neighbour already is — import both, with the neighbour's own hedging intact. (3) **Illusionism lede.** `L95` opens "Illusionists argue Mary learns nothing substantive", which caricatures the position `concepts/illusionism` treats as a spectrum. ⚠️ **Milder than the review claims** — the same sentence immediately grants "a new representational state", so fix the lede clause, do not rewrite the section. **Apply the direct-refutation discipline** for (3): identify what kind of engagement is called for — showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move, or honestly marking a framework-boundary disagreement — and apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** (see [[writing-style]], "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose"). **Do not expose mode labels in the article body**; the classification belongs in the changelog entry. **LENGTH**: article measured 2808 words against concepts soft 2500 / hard 3500 — decompose with `analyze_length` before quoting any figure, and note (2) adds prose ([[analyze-length-counts-reference-apparatus]]). **MIRROR**: sync and grep both trees. — **[ANNOTATED 2026-08-19 from the Gemini 2.5 Pro leg, `reviews/outer-review-2026-08-19-gemini-2-5-pro.md`; no separate task minted, see that file's Verification Notes.]** (4) **NEW — same over-grading failure mode as (1), applied to introspection.** `L133` rests a strong claim on a single 2012 study: "Fox et al. (2012) found that contemplative experience predicts introspective accuracy for subtle phenomenal changes, suggesting introspection is a refinable skill rather than a broken faculty" (**verified verbatim on disk 2026-08-19**). This sentence carries the weight of partitioning introspective unreliability away from phenomenal content — the move the whole section depends on — on one study, in one sensory modality (tactile). Gemini reports null associations for meditation-expertise/introspective-accuracy in adjacent modalities and argues the effect is better read as post-decisional metacognitive artifact than as direct phenomenal access. ⚠️ **Gemini's own supporting citations are UNVERIFIED and two look defective** — its Rahnev & Fleming entry hedges the year "2019/2020" and names the journal "*Neuroscience*" (the paper is in *Neuroscience of Consciousness*), and it attributes "Knowledge of things", *Synthese* 197:3559–3592 to Balog (2020) though title and volume track Matt Duncan, whom its own prose separately credits. **Do not import any of these into the bibliography without publisher-level verification** ([[ai_citation_metadata_unreliable]], [[citation-verify-false-negative]]). The fix that needs no external verification is the **hedge**: downgrade `L133` so one 2012 tactile-discrimination result is not asked to secure phenomenal-content reliability in general. ⚠️ Do NOT delete the Fox citation — it is real and correctly cited; this is a scope-of-claim fix, not a citation defect. **Gemini's two other structural findings on this file were REFUTED on disk and must not be actioned**: its "no epistemic stress-testing of the learning intuition" charge is answered by `L139`, which runs that stress-test and names Schwitzgebel; its "conflates functionalism with physicalism" charge is answered by `L125`, which reads "constrains functionalism **without refuting it**". — **[SYNTHESIS 2026-08-19: upgraded P2 → P1. See `reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-08-19.md`.]** Two of this task's four items are **convergent across both reviewers that returned this cycle**. ⚠️ The Claude leg was abandoned, so this is a **two-of-two convergence in a two-reviewer cycle — a weaker signal than two-of-three**, with no third voice available to expose a shared error ([[convergence-can-be-false-two-reviewers-wrong-one-right]]); both items were therefore re-adjudicated on disk before the upgrade, not after. **(1) is corroborated and independently re-verified.** ChatGPT and Gemini both attack the neuroplasticity promotion, and the source grading was re-checked directly: `topics/clinical-neuroplasticity-evidence-for-bidirectional-causation` `L38` reads "suggestive" and "compatible with", and `L104` states "Removing a defeater is not the same as upgrading the evidence" and that the findings "do not discriminate it from brain-to-brain causation". ⚠️ **Work from ChatGPT's version, not Gemini's**: Gemini's rendering of this charge rests on the **stitched quote** flagged in its own Verification Notes, and additionally attributes "neural correlates, brain lesion studies" to this article — neither appears in it. **(4) is corroborated and was mis-filed as Gemini-only.** ChatGPT independently reached the same Fox et al. finding: "Fox et al. is a preliminary, cross-sectional study... It does not establish the broad proposition that introspection is unreliable only for causal processes while remaining reliable for subtle phenomenal content." The Gemini leg annotated (4) here rather than minting, which was the right call, but recorded it as a single-reviewer novelty; it is two-reviewer. This raises confidence in the **hedge** fix specifically — it does not license importing Gemini's defective citations, which stay barred. **(2) and (3) remain ChatGPT singletons**: still actionable, but not upgraded on convergence grounds and carrying no extra weight. **LENGTH correction**: the note above quotes 2808 words; `analyze_length` measures **2707** on 2026-08-19 (concepts soft 2500 / hard 3500, 793 words of headroom).

### ✓ 2026-08-19: `concepts/knowledge-argument` misdescribes Jackson's 2003 reversal and carries three verified bibliography defects
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/knowledge-argument.md
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-08-19 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro). **ALL SPANS BELOW VERIFIED ON DISK 2026-08-19 — do not re-verify, and note this reviewer produced ZERO fabricated quotes or citations across everything checked, so its factual claims carry unusual weight.** (1) **Jackson 2003 — the reviewer asks for this one "immediately".** `L101` says Jackson embraced physicalism because epiphenomenal qualia "have no causal power" so "we couldn't know about them", then `L103` turns that into support for Bidirectional Interaction. But "Mind and Illusion" rests on **representationalism** and on diagnosing an illusion that confuses intensional properties of representations with properties instantiated by represented objects; Jackson grants Mary new representational states and abilities while denying she discovers a new property. The self-knowledge objection against epiphenomenalism is real but is **not** Jackson's stated reason for changing his mind — attributing it to him converts a physicalist reply into apparent support for interactionism. Fix `L101` to state Jackson's actual argument and relocate the self-knowledge objection to a separately sourced sentence. ⚠️ **SCOPE GUARD: fix the factual characterisation at L101 only.** Do NOT rewrite the framing wholesale — `L167` already concedes that the interactionist reading is "the interactionist's own commitment brought to the argument rather than extracted from it", so the article is not as tenet-blind as the review implies. (2) **Alter, `L194`** reads `Alter, T. (2024). *The Matter of Consciousness*.` OUP confirms the subtitle **"From the Knowledge Argument to Russellian Monism"**, which matters because Alter's destination is Russellian monism, not interactionism — add it. ⚠️ On the year: the review says 2023, we say 2024, and OpenAlex shows Alter's own **2024 *Précis*** of the book plus a 2025 Coleman review — our 2024 probably conflates book with précis. **Confirm at the publisher before editing**; do not swap one unverified year for another ([[figure-disagreement-may-be-two-systems-not-one-error]]). (3) **Bibliography desync, confirmed by count**: Papineau (prose 1 / bib 0), Dennett (4 / 0), Nida-Rümelin (1 / 0), Levine (1 / 0), Schwitzgebel (1 / 0) are invoked in prose with no reference entry; Tallis sits in references with **zero** body uses. Add the five missing entries and either use or relabel Tallis under a separate "Further reading" heading. ⚠️ The reference list is a numbered ledger — re-run a duplicate check after inserting ([[inserting-into-a-numbered-ledger-breaks-cross-references]]). **MIRROR**: sync and grep both `obsidian/` and `hugo/content/` ([[obsidian-only-fix-leaves-defect-live-in-hugo]]). — **[SYNTHESIS 2026-08-19: priority unchanged, context added. See `reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-08-19.md`.]** Item (3)'s bibliography desync is the local face of a finding **both reviewers of this cycle reached independently**: the reference list is frozen pre-2020. Counted on disk 2026-08-19 — **1 of 17 entries post-dates 2016**, and that sole exception is the Alter entry whose year this very task is resolving. That count is context strengthening the case for the five entries already requested here; ⚠️ **it is not a licence to add new literature under this task** — new-citation work is gated in the sibling P2 task, which carries the verification constraints.

### ✓ 2026-08-18: 8 archived URLs redirect by meta-refresh instead of 301 — invisible to the site's stated primary audience
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/workflow/todo.md
- **Notes**: From `/check-links` 2026-08-18. ⚠️ **THE ORIGINATING REPORT SAID "REAL PRODUCTION 404s". THAT IS WRONG — DO NOT INHERIT IT.** Driver-verified: these 8 pages carry Hugo `aliases:` and Hugo **does** build alias pages at the old URLs (`hugo/public/arguments/epiphenomenalism/index.html` exists, emitting `<meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0; url=…/archive/arguments/epiphenomenalism/">`). **Nothing 404s.** **THE REAL DEFECT IS MECHANISM INCONSISTENCY.** `hugo/static/_redirects` carries **510** genuine `301` lines; only **2 of 514** files in `archive/` use `aliases:` at all. These 8 sit outside that convention, so they get a **meta-refresh soft redirect** rather than an HTTP 301. That matters here more than on a normal site: a meta-refresh is honoured by browsers, treated as a soft signal by search engines, and **returns a contentless HTML stub to any client that does not execute the refresh** — including chatbots fetching the URL, which CLAUDE.md names as the Map's primary audience. Same shape as [[noindex-does-not-suppress-the-machine-metadata-surface]]: a mechanism that works for browsers failing the audience this site targets. **CAUSE, established by git archaeology — do not re-derive.** `git log -- archive/concepts/free-will.md` returns **nothing**: these files were never in the repo-root `archive/` tree. Commit `bb89a12042` (2026-02-21, "fix(sync): resolve 8 slug collisions blocking sync") wrote them **directly into `hugo/content/archive/`** and relied on `aliases:` alone. `scripts/build.py:90` calls `generate_redirects(archive_path, …)` against the repo-root `archive/` tree, which has never contained them — hence 514 vs 523 files. **THE FILES**: `arguments/{epiphenomenalism,functionalism,many-worlds,materialism}`, `concepts/{consciousness-and-social-cognition,free-will,phenomenal-value-realism}`, `questions/consciousness-selecting-neural-patterns`. All 8 carry valid `original_path` **and** `superseded_by` (driver-verified, 1/1 each), and none appears in `_redirects` (0/8). **THE FIX**: copy the 8 into `archive/` preserving their subpaths, then regenerate redirects so the existing chain-flattener emits proper 301s (every chain endpoint already resolves to a live page — e.g. `/concepts/free-will/` → … → `/topics/free-will/`). Keep the `aliases:` as belt-and-braces. `scripts/` is operator territory — **run** `build.py`, do not edit it. **VERIFY AFTER**: `grep -c '^/arguments/epiphenomenalism/ ' hugo/static/_redirects` returns 1, and the archive tree counts match at 523/523.

- **Notes**: From literature-drift audit 2026-08-18. **Check 1 tripped hard**: the 11 external citations have median year **2016** against an `ai_modified` of 2026-08-01 — a **10-year lag** versus the configured 5-year threshold. **Check 3 did not trip**: `find_superlative_claims` returns 0, so there is no empirical-record currency drift to chase; do not manufacture one. **Frame-divergence is NOT available here** and was checked explicitly: the article's three sections are (a) uncontested signalling biology, (b) the terminology war, (c) the learning disputes — and the 2021-2026 literature engages all three directly. **Grep-verified at mint**: the file contains **zero citation years >= 2021** (the seven `2026` hits are frontmatter dates and the two Map self-cites). Surnames Segundo-Ortin, Trewavas, Kingsland, Hedrich, Serpell, Ponkshe and Minorsky return **0** hits. Calvo appears only as Calvo 2016; Mallatt only as Feinberg & Mallatt 2016; Scherzer only inside the Böhm et al. 2016 author list; Toyota only as 2018. **ALL DOIs BELOW WERE VERIFIED AT CROSSREF DRIVER-SIDE ON 2026-08-18** (publisher-deposited metadata, not a search index) — do not spend web budget re-verifying their existence ([[citation-verify-false-negative]], [[ai_citation_metadata_unreliable]]).
  **TIER 1 — MUST, and it bears on the article's central contribution.** The article's whole payload is that the skeptics' "no" rests on a neural-necessity premise the Map declines. That reading is drawn **only from Taiz et al. 2019**. In 2021 the debate moved somewhere the article does not follow it:
  1. **Calvo, Baluška & Trewavas (2021), "Integrated information as a possible basis for plant consciousness", *Biochem Biophys Res Commun*** — `10.1016/j.bbrc.2020.10.022` (23c). The inflationary wing's *positive theoretical* move: a **non-neural** route to plant consciousness via IIT, not a terminology claim.
  2. **Mallatt, Taiz, Draguhn, Blatt & Robinson (2021), "Integrated information theory does not make plant consciousness more convincing", *Biochem Biophys Res Commun*** — `10.1016/j.bbrc.2021.01.022` (13c). The same skeptic bloc replying to (1). ⚠️ **THE ASK IS TO CHECK, NOT TO ASSUME**: if this rebuttal argues against plant consciousness on **IIT-internal** grounds rather than from neural necessity, then the article's characterisation of the skeptic case is incomplete — there is a newer skeptic argument that does *not* depend on the premise the Map rejects, and section "Relation to Site Perspective" must acknowledge it as a live rival. Read the paper before deciding which way this cuts; the driver did not read it and is not asserting the outcome.
  3. **Segundo-Ortin & Calvo (2021), "Consciousness and cognition in plants", *WIREs Cognitive Science*** — `10.1002/wcs.1578` (63c). The current standard review of exactly this dispute; the article cites Calvo 2016 where this is now the reference statement.
  **TIER 2 — SHOULD, one or two sentences each.** The article's arc reads as though the terminology war closed in 2019. It did not: 4. **Kingsland & Taiz (2024), "Plant 'intelligence' and the misuse of historical sources as evidence", *Protoplasma*** — `10.1007/s00709-024-01988-1` (6c), with **Trewavas (2024) rebuttal** `10.1007/s00709-024-02005-1` and **Minorsky (2024) response** `10.1007/s00709-024-02002-4`. 5. **Segundo-Ortin & Calvo (2023), "Plant sentience? Between romanticism and denial: Science", *Animal Sentience*** — `10.51291/2377-7478.1772` (31c), a target article with a full commentary symposium (ten Cate `...1795`, Carls-Diamante `...1786`, Plebe `...1797`, Mastinu `...1796`, Tiffin `...1823`). 6. **Serpell & Chaves-Campos (2021), "Memory and habituation to harmful and non-harmful stimuli in a field population of the sensitive plant", *J. Tropical Ecology*** — `10.1017/s0266467421000559` (8c). Bears on the **habituation** half of the learning section (Gagliano 2014 vs Biegler 2018) — a natural-population test. It does **not** bear on the associative-conditioning claim; keep those two disputes distinct, as the article currently does correctly.
  **TIER 3 — OPTIONAL, reference-refresh only, minimal prose.** Updates to the two biology anchors: **Scherzer et al. (2022), *Current Biology*** `10.1016/j.cub.2022.08.051` (49c, flytrap ion-transporter basis of fast action potentials); **Hedrich & Kreuzer (2023), "Demystifying the Venus flytrap action potential", *New Phytologist*** `10.1111/nph.19113` (25c); **Toyota (2024), "Conservation of long-range signaling in land plants via glutamate receptor-like channels", *Plant Cell Physiol*** `10.1093/pcp/pcae034` — same author as the article's 2018 anchor.
  ⚠️ **REJECTED AT CROSSREF — DO NOT ADD**: the flytrap "micronewton mechano-sensors" *Nature Plants* paper (Scherzer, Federle, Al-Rasheid & Hedrich) is **2019**, `10.1038/s41477-019-0465-1`, not a 2020s advance — the driver's recall had it as 2022 and Crossref corrected it. Likewise Mallatt et al. "Debunking a myth: plant consciousness" carries a Crossref issued date of **2020** (`10.1007/s00709-020-01579-w`, 67c) despite the 2021 print issue; it is still uncited and still worth adding on the skeptic axis, but cite it as 2020.
  🔴 **THE CALIBRATION FENCE — THE BINDING CONSTRAINT.** The article's position is precisely hedged: processing **yes**, experience **not on present evidence**, and the skeptics' "no" rests on a physicalist commitment the Map does not share. **More citations must not move the article on the five-tier evidential scale** (*established → strongly supported → realistic possibility → live hypothesis → speculative integration*); see [[project/evidential-status-discipline]]. Tier-1 item (1) is an argument **for** plant consciousness and is the live inflation trap — adding it must not raise the Map's credence that plants are conscious, which stays very low. Where new work supports the article's framing it **constrains rivals**; where it supports a rival it must be **acknowledged as a rival**. Preserve verbatim the hedges at the lede ("on present evidence, no—but the *reason*..."), at "plausible but contested" / "currently unreplicated", and at the closing "What plants change is not the Map's credence..." paragraph ([[condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers]]).
  **LENGTH**: measured at mint — **2337 words**, topics soft 3000 / hard 4000. **663 words of headroom**, unusually roomy, but Tier 1+2 will consume much of it: spend it on Tier 1, compress Tier 2 to single sentences, and let Tier 3 be reference-list entries with little or no new prose. ### ✓ 2026-08-18: adopt a three-layer format and primary-source locator rule for comparative articles (outer-review methodology recommendation)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/project/writing-style.md
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-08-18 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), "Site methodology" recommendations 1, 2 and 7 — the three that are cheap, general, and diagnosed from a defect this review actually demonstrated rather than asserted. **(1) THREE-LAYER FORMAT FOR COMPARATIVE ARTICLES.** Where an article appropriates another tradition, keep three layers distinct and visible: the source tradition's own claim; the live scholarly dispute about it; and the Map's proposed appropriation. The eastern-philosophy audit shows what merging them costs — "property dualism operating within Buddhist flux" and *wu wei* as selector theory are Map reconstructions written in the same voice as historical report, which is how a reader loses the ability to tell exegesis from proposal. **(2) PRIMARY-SOURCE LOCATORS.** For doctrinally load-bearing claims about a primary text, require text, chapter or verse, edition and translator. Prohibit bibliography entries of the "various translations" form; the live example is `topics/eastern-philosophy-consciousness.md:241`. **(3) LITERATURE-SENSITIVE FALSIFIER SECTIONS.** "None has occurred" should distinguish a decisive refutation from a serious live countermodel from an unresolved conceptual objection, and should not be asserted without a documented search. **DELIBERATELY OUT OF SCOPE**: the review's recommendations 3, 5 and 8 (extending the citation ledger to four axes, a mandatory empirical-calibration block, and auto-reopening synthesis articles when a dependency changes) are automation and schema changes, not writing-style guidance. They are operator calls about the pipeline and are **recorded here rather than minted** — do not implement them from this task. **SCOPE FENCE**: edit the guide only; do not sweep the corpus for conformance, which would be an unbudgeted multi-article campaign. **ATTRIBUTION**: this is a project doc — stamp `ai_modified`.

### ✓ 2026-08-18: `concepts/contemplative-epistemology` cites Baird et al. 2014 twice — once with a half-qualifier and once, in the evidence table, as joint support for High trust in exactly the domain where Baird's arm came out null
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/contemplative-epistemology.md
- **Notes**: **FLOOR-RESTORING mint by /replenish-queue (conservative, exact-floor +1, ONE mint, no padding).
