Caps measured live with `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files`: topics **320 (gate) — AT CAP**, concepts 319/320, voids 99/100, positions 15/80 — so no `expand-topic` or `research-topic` chain was mintable and this is the only shape available.
  **THE DEFECT, BOTH LOCI READ VERBATIM ON DISK AT MINT — AND NEITHER NEEDS WEB ACCESS TO ADJUDICATE.** **L62** reads, in full: *"The case for extension rests on convergent evidence. Baird et al. (2014) found meditation training enhanced metacognitive accuracy in memory tasks."* That names the positive domain but omits the perception-null contrast, so it is a **partial** qualifier — materially weaker than a flat omission, but still not the finding. **L103** is the sharper one, and it is a different defect in kind. The `## Degrees of Reliability` table reads: `| Current attended phenomenal states | High | Fox et al. 2012; Baird et al. 2014; microphenomenology convergence |`. It enlists Baird as **joint support for High warranted trust in attended phenomenal states** — when Baird's positive arm was the *memory* domain and its *perception* arm was null, and perception is the domain Fox's comparison sits in. The citation is real and correctly attributed; its **dialectical role is mis-stated**. **Re-frame, do not delete** ([[citation-framing-accuracy-lens]]) — the defect points inward at how the Map uses the paper, not at the paper.
  **THE CORRECTIVE WORDING IS ALREADY ON DISK IN TWO VERIFIED PLACES — COPY THE REGISTER, DO NOT RE-DERIVE IT.** (1) `obsidian/concepts/buddhism-and-dualism` **L56**: *"The enhancement also looks domain-specific: Baird et al. (2014), a randomised training study with an active control, found improved introspective accuracy in a memory but not a perception domain—and perception is the domain Fox's cross-sectional comparison sits in. No direct replication of Fox et al. was located, so this is a neighbouring result, not a failed replication."* (2) `obsidian/topics/phenomenal-authority-and-first-person-evidence` **L166**, landed by commit `e9c86653` roughly an hour before this mint, carries the same move in near-identical words (*"a two-week randomised training program with an active control … a neighbouring controlled result rather than a failed replication"*). Both were read at mint. The register is settled corpus practice; reproducing it here makes three consistent surfaces instead of two-and-a-half.
  **THIS FILE WAS IDENTIFIED BY THE PASS THAT JUST FINISHED, WHICH CORRECTLY DECLINED TO EDIT IT.** The `e9c86653` pass fixed four loci in `phenomenal-authority-and-first-person-evidence` and reported this file rather than touching it, respecting single-file scope ([[multifile_task_dropped_file]]). Its completion note names both L62 and L103 explicitly. This mint is the clean follow-through, not a rediscovery. The originating research note prescribes it independently: `obsidian/research/buddhist-philosophy-of-mind-2020s-literature-2026-08-06.md` **Finding 5** states the Map *"already carries the Baird citation elsewhere while flattening its central qualifier"* and names the fix-by-file hazard by name.
  **⚠️ ENUMERATE BY READING THE SECTION, NOT BY RE-RUNNING `grep Baird` — THERE IS A THIRD SURFACE AND IT IS GREP-INVISIBLE.** Two term-based censuses under-counted asserting surfaces in the last three hours ([[fix-by-file-leaves-string-siblings-live]], [[narrow-grep-zero-is-not-proof-of-absence]]). Here the third surface is the **closing sentence of the same L62 paragraph**: *"The pattern is consistent: training in sustained attention improves the accuracy of first-person reports, with improvement tracking practice hours."* That generalisation is built on the convergent set **including the flattened Baird**, and a `Baird` grep will never see it. Read `### Trained Attention` (L57–L64) and `## Degrees of Reliability` (L96–L108) **in full**, top to bottom, and decide surface-by-surface; do not trust any string search — including the strings quoted in this note — as the enumeration. Note that **L64** already hedges well (*"The evidence is strongest for attention-adjacent discriminations"*); that sentence is probably fine as-is and should not be padded.
  **⚠️ DO NOT RUN A NAIVE CORPUS-WIDE `Baird` SWEEP — MOST HITS ARE DIFFERENT PAPERS.** Six content articles cite "Baird" and **four cite unrelated work**: `topics/dream-consciousness` and `concepts/metacognition` cite **Baird et al. 2018** (aPFC / lucid dreaming), `topics/lucid-dreaming-and-dualist-rendering` cites **2019**, `topics/incubation-effect-and-unconscious-processing` cites **2012** (mind-wandering). **Only the 2014 metacognition paper is in scope. Check the year before touching anything.** This task is single-file regardless: `obsidian/concepts/contemplative-epistemology.md` only.
  **CALIBRATION CEILING — DO NOT EXCEED WHAT THE SOURCES LICENSE.** The research note's evidence-grade table (**L61**) files Baird et al. 2014 under **"C — metadata verified only … content not read"**. Bibliographic metadata and the quoted abstract span are solid; the full paper has not been read. Confine any addition to the domain contrast and the descriptors in the quoted abstract span: *two-week randomised program*, *active control*, *memory positive, perception null*. **No effect sizes, no sample size, no follow-up interval, no mechanism.** And frame it as a **neighbouring controlled result, never a refutation of Fox** — two designs measuring different things can both be right, and the note says so in terms. Preserve the existing hedges at L59 (*"promising but indirect"*, *"The inferential gap must be acknowledged"*) — do not trade one calibration for another ([[condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers]]).
  **THE FIX — TWO SURGICAL EDITS PLUS A JUDGEMENT CALL, ~40–60 WORDS TOTAL.** (a) **L62**: extend the Baird sentence with the perception-null contrast and the neighbouring-result framing, in the L56/L166 register. (b) **L103**: the table cell must stop presenting Baird as unqualified joint support for High trust in attended phenomenal states — either move the citation to a row it actually supports, or qualify it in place (e.g. naming the memory domain), whichever preserves the table's readability; the row's `High` rating itself rests on Fox plus microphenomenology convergence and need not change. ### ✓ 2026-08-18: eastern-philosophy-consciousness has zero engagement with active inference — the literature that models the very states it reads as evidence for dualism
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/eastern-philosophy-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-08-18, 2/3 reviewers): [claude, gemini]. **Upgraded P2 → P1 by combine-outer-reviews.** Synthesis: `reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-08-18.md`. **Gemini 2.5 Pro** (section 5) found the identical gap independently and supplies a *different* verified paper for it — Laukkonen, R. & Slagter, H. (2021), "From many to (n)one: Meditation and the plasticity of the predictive mind", *Neuroscience & Biobehavioral Reviews*, DOI `10.1016/j.neubiorev.2021.06.021` (confirmed real at collect time). Its verdict: "The author's failure to engage with the predictive processing model of meditation renders the entire section on contemplative evidence a relic of a bygone era in neuroscience." Two independent legs, two independently verified papers, one grep-verified gap — this is the cycle's cleanest 2/3 convergence. **Laukkonen & Slagter 2021 is the better citation for the meditation-specific claim** than the 2025 *Beautiful Loop* paper, whose fit to cessation and minimal phenomenal experience was the reviewer's inference rather than the authors' claim. The do-not-propagate warning below stands unchanged. Originating leg's brief follows. — From outer review 2026-08-18 (Claude Opus 5), section (d)(iii). **THE GAP IS REAL AND TOTAL, GREP-VERIFIED AT MINT**: a case-insensitive search of this article for `friston|active inference|predictive processing|laukkonen` returns **zero**. The article discusses *nirodha samāpatti*, minimal phenomenal experience and Buddhist momentariness — all of which now have explicit active-inference models — and treats the contemplative phenomenology as evidential pressure toward dualism without registering that a naturalistic computational programme addresses the same phenomena. No open task covers this; the only Laukkonen entry in this queue is a **closed** 2026-06-20 author-vector metadata fix on a different citation, so this is not a duplicate. **THE SOURCE IS VERIFIED**: Laukkonen, R., Friston, K., & Chandaria, S. (2025). "A beautiful loop: An active inference theory of consciousness." *Neuroscience and Biobehavioral Reviews*, **176**, 106296. Confirmed at Europe PMC and by DOI resolution (`10.1016/j.neubiorev.2025.106296` → Elsevier PII `S0149763425002970`) — title, all three authors, journal, volume, year and article number all check out exactly. Open access. ⚠️ **DO NOT PROPAGATE THE REVIEWER'S QUOTE.** The review claims the abstract says the theory "explains altered states like meditation, psychedelics, and minimal states". **It does not.** The abstract's actual closing sentence, transcribed from the record, is: "This Beautiful Loop Theory is also deeply revealing about altered states, meditation, and the full spectrum of conscious experience." Neither "psychedelics" nor "minimal states" appears in it, and "deeply revealing about" is materially weaker than "explains". Cite the paper for what it claims — altered states and meditation — and do **not** assert that it models cessation or MPE specifically unless you verify that in the body of the paper first. **THE ASK**: add a compact engagement, not a survey. One paragraph that names active inference as the leading naturalistic competitor for the contemplative phenomena this article leans on, states honestly what it would explain if it succeeds, and marks where the Map thinks it falls short — in the register the article already uses for illusionism at `:132` ("evidential pressure rather than proof"), which is the right calibration and should be matched rather than exceeded. **SECOND LOCUS, OPTIONAL AND SECONDARY**: `obsidian/concepts/neurophenomenology-and-contemplative-neuroscience.md` is the natural home for the fuller treatment if this article proves length-bound — check `analyze_length` before adding, and prefer routing a wikilink there over inflating this file. **RELATED BUT UNVERIFIED**: the review also asserts Metzinger models MPE as "a Bayesian representation of tonic alertness" (*Philosophy and the Mind Sciences* 1(I), 2020). ### ✓ 2026-08-18: eastern-philosophy-consciousness omits Siderits 2025 "Buddhist Physicalism?" and its falsifier list still says "None has occurred"
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/eastern-philosophy-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-08-18, 3/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude, gemini]. **Upgraded P2 → P1 by combine-outer-reviews.** Synthesis: `reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-08-18.md`. All three legs independently named the same missing book, and two verified it at separate indexes (ChatGPT at OpenAlex, Gemini at Crossref). **Claude Opus 5** frames it as author-stance rather than omission: "Siderits is a Buddhist Reductionist now defending *Buddhist Physicalism?* (OUP 2025); runs no-self toward physicalism, not dualism" — i.e. the article cites his 2007 introduction while his 2025 monograph runs against the thesis it is cited for. **Gemini 2.5 Pro**: "They completely ignore Mark Siderits's highly anticipated 2025 monograph." Originating leg's brief follows. — From outer review 2026-08-18 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), sections 6–7. **THE SOURCE IS REAL — VERIFIED, DO NOT RE-LITIGATE ITS EXISTENCE.** OpenAlex returns *Buddhist Physicalism?* as a 2025 book by Mark Siderits, DOI `10.1093/9780197799697.001.0001`, and the OUP page title confirms the subtitle *Non-self Metaphysics and Phenomenal Consciousness*. Its chapter list includes "The Problem of Phenomenal Consciousness", "Assessing the Plausibility of a Buddhist Physicalism", "Meta-Cognition and the Reflexivity Thesis" and "Consciousness in Early Buddhism and the Abhidharma Schools". The article's bibliography at `:236` carries only **Siderits 2007** (*Buddhism as Philosophy*). **WHY IT MATTERS MORE THAN A MISSING CITE.** The article's illusionism section treats illusionism as an external threat to Buddhism's practical project. Siderits 2025 supplies a **Buddhist-internal** route to it — inner mirroring from first-person theory of mind, the subject-object dichotomy as illusory, qualia as artifacts of a useful fiction — which the review says refutes the framing that illusionism only presses from outside. Note the title's question mark and the "Assessing the Plausibility" chapter: Siderits presents the physicalist reconstruction as exploratory, not settled, so engage it as a live countermodel rather than as a defeat. **THE FALSIFIER CLAIM.** `:163`–`:170` lists five falsifiers and closes "None has occurred. The engagement remains productive." Two entries need re-calibration against what is now known. Falsifier 2 ("Illusionism explains contemplative reports") faces a developed Buddhist-internal countermodel. Falsifier 4 ("Cessation states prove to be mere unconsciousness") is subtler than a flat no: the cessation preprint's authors say consciousness *ceased*, which affirms the first half, while their finding of no global suppression means the neural signature is *not* that of ordinary unconsciousness, which is what the falsifier's wording actually tests. Say that precisely. Per the review's methodology point, distinguish a decisive refutation from a serious live countermodel from an unresolved conceptual objection, rather than collapsing all three into "None has occurred". **CURRENCY**: interpretive sections still rest on Siderits 2007, Garfield 1995 and Gupta 1998; adding current specialist work on Śaṅkara and Daoism is in scope if headroom allows. **ATTRIBUTION**: hold the file's existing `ai_system`. **MIRROR**: sync and verify in `hugo/content/`.

### ✓ 2026-08-18: eastern-philosophy-consciousness flattens Advaita, Buddhism and Daoism into one convergence claim — differentiate by school and reclassify Advaita as a rival
- **Type**: refine-draft

- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-08-18, 3/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude, gemini]. **Upgraded P2 → P1 by combine-outer-reviews** — the cycle's strongest convergence; all three legs independently identified the same structural defect. Synthesis: `reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-08-18.md`. **Claude Opus 5**: "The convergence is manufactured by selective reading, so **evidential-independence fails**: the traditions are not independent witnesses but the same tenet-set ventriloquised three times"; **Gemini 2.5 Pro**: "systematically flattening complex, anti-foundationalist Eastern ontologies to force them into a parochial Western Cartesian-Whiteheadian framework". Claude adds a locus ChatGPT did not: the Advaita **Tension** paragraph at `:72` already states the incompatibility and then dissolves it by fiat — "confession-without-correction" — so the fix is not to add the objection but to stop bracketing the one already there. ⚠️ **Gemini's version of the Advaita charge was partly declined**: it quotes only the "Alignment" paragraph and passes over the "Tension" paragraph directly beneath it, so do not inherit the claim that the article never noticed the problem. Originating leg's brief follows. — From outer review 2026-08-18 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), sections 1–4. The review's structural charge: the convergence thesis is stated at a level ("consciousness is real and significant") compatible with physicalism, idealism, neutral monism and Madhyamaka conventionalism alike, so it cannot carry weight for interactionist dualism. **THREE CONCRETE ASKS.** (a) **Advaita** — `:72` says Advaita's eternal *ātman* is "more comfortable with substance dualism than the Map's agnostic stance" (verified verbatim). The review argues this is misleading: *ātman* is identical with nondual Brahman, so Advaita supplies no individual conscious substance alongside a material one, and at the ultimate level there are not two relata for Bidirectional Interaction at all. Reclassify Advaita as a **nondual anti-physicalist rival** rather than a dualism-friendly ally, and drop the claim that the two-levels-of-truth doctrine *resolves* the interaction problem — it dissolves the problem by denying the independence of the relata. (b) **Buddhism** — `:80` asserts "property dualism operating within Buddhist flux" with "Buddhism" undifferentiated. Split the treatment by school: early aggregate analysis, Abhidharma realism about mental events, Yogācāra idealism, Madhyamaka anti-foundationalism. The review's point is that as written, "Buddhism" functions as a container from which whichever element fits the Map is selected. (c) **Daoism** — `:100` asserts the *Tao* is "fundamental reality... crucially a process rather than a substance" and reads *wu wei* as bearing on the witness/selector problem. The review holds this is Map-generated analogy, not sourced Daoist philosophy, and notes that on prominent readings Zhuangzi's distributed bodily agency tells *against* a privileged witness-selector. Either ground it in specific Laozi/Zhuangzi passages or relabel the section explicitly as speculative comparison. **HONOUR [[evidential-status-discipline]]** — cross-tradition resonance is suggestive context, not independent evidence for the tenets; that discipline is the whole point of the review's section 1. **LENGTH**: this file is a survey; check headroom with `analyze_length` before adding, and prefer replacing over appending. **ATTRIBUTION**: hold the file's existing `ai_system`. **MIRROR**: sync and verify in `hugo/content/`.

### ✓ 2026-08-18: eastern-philosophy-consciousness reads the extended-cessation study backwards — its authors say consciousness ceased — and the Fox et al. correction never reached the inference
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/eastern-philosophy-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-08-18, 2/3 reviewers on the Fox strand): [chatgpt, claude]. **Already P1 — no upgrade available; convergence recorded.** Synthesis: `reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-08-18.md`. **Claude Opus 5** (citation table, row 2) reached the same verdict independently: the study is "cross-sectional (experience *predicts* accuracy; does not show training *produces* it)", graded FAIL on inference fidelity. **Gemini 2.5 Pro** pressed the same sentence on different grounds ("Meditation does not \"refine phenomenal access\" to an irreducible bedrock reality, as the manuscript falsely claims based on 2012 data"), but its framing that the article "relies entirely on Fox" was declined at collect time — do not inherit it. ⚠️ **The cessation-reversal half of this task is a ChatGPT singleton** — no sibling leg found that the preprint's authors classify the state as consciousness ceasing. It is verified at Europe PMC and stands on its own evidence, not on convergence. Originating leg's brief follows. — From outer review 2026-08-18 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro), section 6. Two empirical-fidelity defects on one file; both verified at primary source by the collector, so treat them as confirmed rather than as candidates. **(1) CESSATION — THE INFERENCE IS REVERSED.** `:118` reads: "*Nirodha samāpatti*—complete cessation of perception and feeling—can be voluntarily achieved while brain activity continues. This challenges the assumption that consciousness *is* neural activity, supporting the filter theory." The cited work is the Sacchet-lab extended-cessation preprint, whose Europe PMC record gives the title "Endogenous suspension and reset of consciousness: 7T fMRI brain mapping of the extended cessation meditative endpoint" (Yang WF, Kadambi A, Abellaneda-Perez K, Mackin G, Beslic I, Potash R, Sparby T, Sacchet MD). Its abstract describes a state "in which consciousness is volitionally **suspended**", reports **N=3**, and concludes that "**consciousness can cease** without global suppression" — challenging Global Neuronal Workspace and IIT while supporting the Active Inference Framework. Residual regional activity during a state the authors classify as *unconscious* does not show consciousness continuing independently of neural activity. Rewrite to the supportable claim: the study challenges theories predicting a specific global signature for unconsciousness; it does not support filter theory or dualism. **(2) THE CLAIM IS ALSO UNCITED** — the preprint appears nowhere in the reference list at `:225`–`:242`. Add it, marked as a preprint with its N. **(3) FOX ET AL. — THE CORRECTION STOPPED AT THE CITATION.** `:132` correctly uses the repaired wording "Fox et al. (2012) showed meditation **experience predicts** introspective accuracy", then immediately reverts in the inference: "If phenomenal consciousness were illusion, **training** should eventually *dissolve* it. Instead, **training** *refines* phenomenal access." Fox et al. was a cross-sectional comparison of meditators differing in experience, not a training study, so the causal verb is not licensed. Carry the correction into the inference, not just the citation sentence. **SCOPE FENCE**: this task is empirical-inference calibration only. Do not re-open the illusionism argument itself — that is the sibling P2 on Siderits 2025 and the falsifier list. **ATTRIBUTION**: hold the file's existing `ai_system`. **MIRROR**: sync and verify in `hugo/content/`.

  2. `reviews/deep-review-2026-06-15-composition-question-rivals.md` **L36** checked the merged text against the *archived originals* ("character-identical to the archived original") — an intra-corpus identity check, not a source check.

  **INDEPENDENT CONFIRMATION THAT NO REVIEW ANYWHERE HAS REPRODUCED THESE SPANS.** `grep -ric` for `straightforward conceptual and/or empirical work` across all **7226** files in `obsidian/reviews/` returns **zero non-zero hits**; same for `variability or plasticity`.   **THE FOUR UNCHECKED SPANS, WITH LINE NUMBERS VERIFIED AT MINT.**
  1. **L53** — Thomasson, *Ontology Made Easy* (2015): existence questions "are answered by `"straightforward conceptual and/or empirical work"` rather than by distinctively philosophical metaphysics."
  2. **L53** — Thomasson: the easy approach infers "by `"trivial"` steps". Single word in quotation marks; low risk, but it is doing attributive work.
  3. **L55** — Thomasson: the hard Quinean dispute over whether tables `"really"` exist "is `"misguided."`" Note the terminal period sits *inside* the quotation marks, so what is being asserted as Thomasson's word may be "misguided" or "misguided." — resolve which.
  4. **L63** — Hirsch, *Quantifier Variance and Realism* (2011): the quantificational apparatus "has, **in Hirsch's words**, `"a certain variability or plasticity."`" The explicit "in Hirsch's words" makes this the strongest verbatim commitment in the article and the highest-value single check.

The likely remedy if the review's rendering holds is an ellipsis or a re-cut of the quoted span, **not** deletion ([[citation-verify-false-negative]], [[citation-framing-accuracy-lens]]).

### ✓ 2026-08-17: `apex/ai-as-introspection-control` — the gameability sorting quarantines the channel its flagship designed test requires, plus three calibration slips
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/apex/ai-as-introspection-control.md
- **Notes**: First-ever pessimistic review of this article (4517w, `soft_warning`, 517w over apex soft 4000 — **the refine must be length-neutral or reducing**; issue 3's fix reduces, issues 2 and 4 are neutral, issue 1 needs the L91 softmax gloss trimmed to fund its clause). Four issues, all verified `grep -cF` == 1 against the raw file. **(1) HIGH — L115 vs L91/L95.** L91 calls confidence calibration "the sharpest affordance" and L95 builds the article's one *designed* discriminator on it, reproducing the four ingredients of [[topics/anti-correlation-probes-for-ai-consciousness]] including "own-channel confidence report", read as "whether the system's *reported* confidence inverts". L115 then declares "Verbal confidence and volunteered provenance sit on the gameable side, corrupted at the source; the affordances that carry the argument are the *ungameable* internal ones—a source tag an experimenter sets to a known-wrong value, a confidence distribution read off activations, an integrating trace recovered by interpretability". But anti-correlation is a *relation between* a report and the internal evidence it fails to track — read confidence off activations and you have measured the internal-evidence side, leaving no report to invert against it. The remedy dissolves the affordance it is meant to harden. Worse, the source article names this as an open problem (`anti-correlation-probes-for-ai-consciousness.md` L122, "**The own-report problem**… a verbally-reported confidence, a token probability, and a fine-tuned calibration head are different signals… and that choice is not yet principled") and the apex carries **no trace of it** — `own-report`, `calibration head`, `not yet principled` all grep zero — while L91 settles it by fiat toward the token-probability option ("confidence outputs—softmax distributions, log-probabilities—are externally inspectable"), which the source explicitly distinguishes from "the system's own report, not a post-hoc statistical wrapper". NOTE: the 2026-07-19 deep review **endorsed** this passage ("correctly sorts affordances by gameability… a restraint move, not an upgrade", L45) — a prior ratification, so do not assume it was considered. FIX: exempt the designed anti-correlation test from the blanket verbal-confidence quarantine, stating that its gameability is handled by the source article's named controls (held-out vocabularies, adversarially-trained self-report regimes, activation-patching, cross-model feature comparison, preregistered negative controls) rather than by discarding the channel; then import the own-report caveat in one clause. **(2) MEDIUM-HIGH — missing third disjunct.** L63/L79/L103/L107/L152 treat "bounded witness" vs "bounded self-model" as exhaustive (L107: they "agree on every human observation and disagree on what a legible system should show"). A third region — opacity that is architecture-specific *and* witnessless (evolved biological self-models, developmental history, wetware bandwidth) — is never named, and it is exactly where L103's negative branch should send its credit. L103's "the human inversion is more architecturally specific than the generic reading allows" is valid; the following "and the bounded-witness reading earns a corresponding (small) credit" does not follow from it. The "(small)" parenthetical hedges *magnitude*, not the *structural* gap. FIX: name the third region once at L79; adjust L103 so credit flows to "the inversion is architecture-specific rather than generic", marking the further step to the witness reading as needing an argument not supplied here; soften L107's exhaustiveness. **(3) MEDIUM-HIGH — L87 asserts what L101 conditionalises.** L87 runs "the architecture does not preclude logging it" → "A system that writes source tags at encoding would not have the source-attribution void" → "That a system *can* be built without the void is **the cleanest evidence** that the human void is a fact about us, not about self-modelling as such", and states flatly "Storytelling and origin-tagging are not architecturally inevitable; they are inevitable *for our architecture*". No such system exists; L87's own preceding sentence records that current systems confabulate provenance. L101 states the same claim correctly — "If instead source-logging architectures are **demonstrably buildable and lose nothing essential**, the human void is shown to be contingent" — with the antecedent, the demonstration requirement, and the substantive "loses nothing essential" clause L87 lacks. That clause is the argument: a system with an external provenance log has not thereby acquired a self-model with access to it, and the generic reading quantifies over self-models ("the self-model is a low-dimensional summary, and summaries discard their derivation", L79), not over databases. FIX: rewrite L87's last two sentences into L101's conditional form, carrying the reference-class requirement; delete the "and this is the load-bearing point" intensifier. **(4) MEDIUM — L63 lead.** "for the first time, externally inspectable" ranges unqualified over all four affordances, at the truncation-critical surface. The body qualifies it twice (L93 choice blindness "approximates it from outside"; L171 pupillometry as "The biological near-analogue to instrumented AI source-tagging"), and the anchor study itself externally manipulated the internal variable *in humans* — Rebouillat et al. "used a choice-blindness paradigm with a brain-computer interface to manipulate the strength of the internal decision evidence available to participants". The **narrow** L93 claim (no knob on the brain's source-monitoring an experimenter can set to a known-wrong value) survives and must be preserved; only the lead's generalisation of it overreaches. FIX: replace "for the first time" with a resolution/manipulability qualification. FREE RIDERS if budget allows: the flagged "This is not X. It is Y." construct sits at four high-visibility surfaces (description L3, apex_thesis L55, lead L63, heading L109) — at minimum reword the L109 heading; and "J-space" is forward-referenced at L127 sixteen lines before its L143 introduction with no named anchor. **EXPLICITLY NOT IN SCOPE**: the Gurnee reference form at L182 (the 2026-07-19 deep review already ruled it faithful to the announcement URL it cites; residual is cosmetic), and the missing J-space venue/replication caveat at L143 (a corpus-wide pattern, not an article-specific lapse — do not fix here in isolation).

  1. ⚠️ **HIGH — §Direction 3 quotes a deleted sentence.** The article attributes to P-Q9: *"…it is not a global unfalsifiability shield. The framework retains real empirical exposure at the mechanism level."* The second sentence was removed from the register on **2026-07-30, commit `72ebf715f8`**. It is **STALE, NOT FABRICATED** — `git log -S` confirms it was added under `9a3a1a433a` and genuinely existed. Re-quote from the current register; do not retract the point. Then either drop the expected-value phrasing or add an explicit tractability term. **Note this under-count runs AGAINST the Map** — it under-sells Direction 2 — so correct it in that direction rather than trimming Direction 1.

### ✓ 2026-08-17: the report-latency research landed as "fold, do not spend the last voids slot" and warned it would become a third orphaned note without a task — ship the fold into its measured hosts

  **THE NAV-SURFACE DEFECT.** **L3** `description:` — "Consciousness causally enables the cognitive leap that separates humans from great apes" — states as settled what **L109** immediately hedges: the interface-threshold model "predicts bounded cognition below the threshold and a cluster of new capabilities appearing together… a pattern gradual amplification also predicts if returns accelerate sharply enough, so the clustering does not by itself decide between the two models." **L71** likewise runs the strong causal claim in the body. The `description:` is what search engines and chatbots surface; fix the label as well as the prose ([[navigation-surfaces-carry-unreviewed-claims]]).
  **THE UNDERDETERMINATION.** The comparative ape–human pattern is consistent with at least three readings the article does not separate: phenomenal consciousness *causing* the capacities; phenomenal consciousness *accompanying* a physical access architecture; and metarepresentation producing both sophisticated cognition and reports of experience. The article treats the first as the mechanism rather than as one underdetermined reading. The neighbouring `concepts/theory-of-mind` is more careful — it recognises implicit false-belief competence in great apes and live behaviour-reading alternatives — so the corpus already contains the corrective.
  **APPLY THE DIRECT-REFUTATION DISCIPLINE.** Identify what kind of engagement this calls for: showing the rival reading is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move, or honestly marking a framework-boundary disagreement. Here the honest move is likely the third plus a discriminator proposal — separate phenomenal consciousness, global access, explicit metarepresentation, language, executive control and cumulative culture, then state what observation would distinguish phenomenal causation from a common neural cause of both phenomenality and cognition. Write this in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [[writing-style|the writing-style guide]]'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body**; the classification belongs in the changelog entry. If the in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, say in plain language that the disagreement sits closer to bedrock than it first appeared.

### ✓ 2026-08-17: three verified citation defects this cycle share one root cause — no citation records what kind of evidence it is
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/project/writing-style.md
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-08-17 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro full-site audit), methodological improvement 4. This is a methodology proposal, minted because the audit's three independently verified citation defects all instantiate it.
  **THE EVIDENCE FOR THE PROPOSAL.** All three defects verified on disk this cycle are the same shape — a source's *kind* was silently upgraded: **PBR** (a theorem conditional on preparation independence) read as an unqualified metaphysical result; **Denton et al. 2024** (a simulation identifying parameter regions) read as "working biology"; **Killingsworth & Gilbert** (experience sampling of task-unrelated thought) read as a measurement of narrative-self processing. None was a fabricated reference. The reviewer's summary is apt: the Map's recurring citation problem is **scope inflation and construct substitution rather than fabricated bibliography** — which matters, because metadata-checking lenses are blind to it ([[quote-fidelity-defects-survive-metadata-reviews]], [[empirical-claim-fidelity-orthogonal-to-metadata-and-quotes]]).
  **THE PROPOSAL.** Require each load-bearing citation to record what kind of evidence it is — experiment, simulation, toy model, theorem, review, preprint, or philosophical argument — together with the decisive assumptions it rests on. Add this to the writing-style guide's citation section as a stated standard, and note the corollary the reviewer draws: a claim whose parent is a Grade-D, programme-stage or none-by-construction commitment should not acquire categorical wording or a higher external-evidence grade without a separately identified source of evidence.
  **SCOPE GUARD.** Write the standard; **do not attempt a corpus-wide retrofit** — that would be a multi-hundred-file sweep. Sibling tasks in this batch fix the three known instances. Keep the addition short and concrete enough to be checkable by a later audit lens.

On the preferred route the categorical sentence does not follow.

Separate two claims the paragraph currently merges: (a) the *abstract computation* is classical — true, and the "quantum fossil" analysis at L149 supports it; (b) *no physical event in the hardware* satisfies the eligibility condition — unsupported while no eligibility law exists. The honest verdict is that current AI is unlikely to qualify **on the Map's preferred route pending an eligibility criterion**, not that there is categorically nothing to collapse.

### ✓ 2026-08-17: two articles attribute unrestricted in-principle detectability to Tenet 3, and today's pessimistic review adjudicated the tension the wrong way
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/epistemology-of-mechanism-at-the-consciousness-matter-interface.md
- **Notes**: **ALL THREE LOCI VERIFIED VERBATIM ON DISK 2026-08-16 with `grep -cF` (count 1 each), and each is mirrored in `hugo/content/` — sync after editing, do not leave the hugo tree stale.**
  **THE DEFECT.** `tenets.md` L81 scopes the detection falsifier explicitly: direct detection of a consciousness-correlated Born-statistics deviation "bites **only on minimum-outside-corridor readings**, since the corridor reading the Map endorses is constructed to leave the aggregate Born measure intact and is, by that construction, indistinguishable from chance." L75 adds that this is "not a near-term test awaiting better equipment but a framework-boundary feature." Tenet 3's own text commits to outcome-selection influence and says nothing about detectability. Three loci assert it unrestrictedly:
  (Z1) `epistemology-of-mechanism…` **L123** — *"If consciousness causes physical effects, those effects are in principle detectable, and the mechanism is in principle discoverable—even if current methods are insufficient."* The source locus and the stronger claim: it adds mechanism-discoverability and closes the escape the corridor reading needs.
  (Z2) `judging-the-map-as-science` **L91** — *"the physical effects consciousness has are in principle detectable, which is what Tenet 3 commits the Map to"*.
  (Z3) `judging-the-map-as-science` **L143** — *"if consciousness causes physical effects, the effects are in principle detectable, and the programme cannot claim progressiveness indefinitely while its mechanism stays empirically silent."*
  **THE CORPUS NORM IS ESTABLISHED — copy it, do not compose.** `apex/self-concealing-interface` L165 states the contradictory in Map voice: *"The concealment is **entailed** by this tenet."* `concepts/ensemble-level-epiphenomenalism` L58 and `concepts/coupling-modes` L116 scope detectability precisely to the *deviation* route. Eight further articles run the corridor / minimum-outside-corridor distinction explicitly.
  **FIX.** Scope all three to minimum-outside-corridor readings; on the endorsed corridor reading the influence is constructed to leave the aggregate Born measure intact, so the standing obligation is to produce discriminating structure rather than to await better instruments. At Z2 also drop *"which is what Tenet 3 commits the Map to"* — Tenet 3 commits to outcome-selection influence, not to its detectability.
  **DO NOT WEAKEN Z3's ACCOUNTABILITY CLAUSE.** "cannot claim progressiveness indefinitely while its mechanism stays empirically silent" is correct, runs against the Map, and survives the scoping intact. The edit narrows *what kind* of detection is owed, not whether the programme is accountable.
  **⚠️ DO NOT FOLLOW THE PESSIMISTIC REVIEW ON THIS.** `reviews/pessimistic-2026-08-16-judging-the-map-as-science.md` L83 found this exact tension and resolved it *toward* detectability; the refine executed at 11:39Z took that advice and deleted the phrase that gestured at concealment (`"no publicly accessible causal effects of the right kind"` now greps zero in the apex, surviving only at its source `topics/consciousness-and-the-problem-of-measurement-standards` L65). That review's line numbers (L85/L135) are stale and its Counterargument 1 is discharged. This task reverses the direction of that adjudication.
